#/bin/bash

# 蛻晏ｧ句喧
init(){
    cfg_init
    cfg_check

    # 闃らせ蟾･菴懃岼蠖・    ROOT=${cfg['root']}
    # 莉｣遐∝ｺ捺園螟・岼蠖包ｼ御ｾ・ /data/game.dev
    CODE_PATH=${cfg['code_path']}/${cfg['ver']}

    # nginx陌壽供荳ｻ譛ｺ驟咲ｽｮ蟄俶叛逶ｮ蠖・    if [ -d "/data/conf/nginx/vhost" ]; then
        VHOST_PATH="/data/conf/nginx/vhost"
    else
        VHOST_PATH="/usr/local/webserver/nginx/conf/vhost"
    fi
    if [ "${cfg['combine']}" = "" ]; then
        cfg['combine']='<<"center_0">>'
    fi
    # VHOST_PATH="/etc/nginx/sites-enabled"
    if [ "${cfg['combine']}" = "" ]; then
        cfg['combine']='<<"center_0">>'
    fi

    # erl遞句ｺ乗園蝨ｨ霍ｯ蠕・    ERL=erl
    if [ -f /opt/erlang/otp19.2/lib/erlang/bin/erl ]; then
        ERL=/opt/erlang/otp19.2/lib/erlang/bin/erl
    elif [ -f /usr/local/erlang19/bin/erl ]; then
        ERL=/usr/local/erlang19/bin/erl
    fi
    # erl闃らせ髣ｴ霑樊磁遶ｯ蜿｣闌・峩
    ERL_PORT_MIN=40100
    # erl闃らせ髣ｴ霑樊磁遶ｯ蜿｣闌・峩
    ERL_PORT_MAX=44000

    # 霑帛・闃らせ譬ｹ逶ｮ蠖・    cd ${ROOT} || exit 1
}

# 驟咲ｽｮ菫｡諱ｯ譽譟･
cfg_check(){
    if [ -e "${cfg['root']}" ]; then
        return
    else if [ "${DEBUG}" != "1" ]; then
            echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]閼壽悽譛ｪ霑幄｡梧ｭ｣遑ｮ驟咲ｽｮ・悟ｦよ棡蟶梧悍莉･蠑蜿第ｨ｡蠑丞ｷ･菴懆ｯｷ蟆・EBUG隶ｾ鄂ｮ荳ｺ'1'・悟ｹｶ蝪ｫ蜀吝・髞ｮ驟咲ｽｮ"
            exit 1
        fi
    fi
    # 闔ｷ蜿門ｽ灘燕閼壽悽譁・ｻｶ謇蝨ｨ霍ｯ蠕・ｼ御ｽ應ｸｺ譬ｹ逶ｮ蠖・    SOURCE="${BASH_SOURCE[0]}"
    while [ -h "$SOURCE" ]; do
[REDACTED SENSITIVE LINE]
        SOURCE="$(readlink "$SOURCE")"
        [[ $SOURCE != /* ]] && SOURCE="$DIR/$SOURCE"
    done
[REDACTED SENSITIVE LINE]

    # 隹・ｯ墓ｨ｡蠑丈ｸ狗噪鮟倩ｮ､驟咲ｽｮ・悟ｦよ怏蠢・ｦ∬ｯｷ謇句勘菫ｮ謾ｹ
    # cygwin荳矩怙菴ｿ逕ｨdos譬ｼ蠑乗燕閭ｽ豁｣蟶ｸ・悟ｦ・d:/game.dev/zone/mxfx_local_1
    cfg['root']="${root}"
    # cygwin荳矩怙菴ｿ逕ｨdos譬ｼ蠑乗燕閭ｽ豁｣蟶ｸ・悟ｦ・d:/game.dev
    cfg['code_path']="/data/game.dev"
    cfg['nodename']="s1@local.dev"
    cfg['platform']="local"
    cfg['zone_id']="0"
    cfg['type']="zone"
    cfg['host']="local.dev"
    cfg['port']="8000"
    cfg['cookie']="n2su1x83b16,2uw2"
    cfg['srv_key']="a2bi2Zc89n129BaE3N0"
    cfg['other_key']="a2bi2Zc89n129BaE3N0"
    cfg['game_name']="mxfx"
    cfg['zone_name']="譌ｶ遨ｺ逾槫沺蠑蜿第恪"
    cfg['lang']="zh_CN"
    cfg['fcm']="0"
    cfg['db_host']="127.0.0.1"
    cfg['db_port']="3306"
    cfg['db_name']="mxfx_local_1"
    cfg['db_user']="root"
    cfg['db_pass']="Android"
    cfg['db_conn_min']="10"
    cfg['db_conn_max']="30"
    cfg['ip']="127.0.0.1"
    cfg['open_time']="1384853886"
    cfg['website']="http://sksy.cc"

    # 譖ｿ謐｢遨ｺ蜿倬㍼
    for k in ${!cfg[@]}; do
        cfg[$k]=${cfg[$k]/\{\{*\}\}/}
    done
}

# 蜷ｯ蜉ｨ闃らせ
fun_start(){
    if [ ! -e dets ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]蠖灘燕逶ｮ蠖穂ｸ区悴螳芽｣・鰍轤ｹ・瑚ｯｷ蜈域鴬陦悟ｮ芽｣・桃菴・
        exit 1
    fi
    if [ $(screen -ls | grep "${cfg['nodename']}" | wc -l ) -le 0 ]; then  #蛛懈恪譖ｴ譁ｰ譌ｶ蟇ｹ譌･蠢怜★謳ｬ遘ｻ螟・炊
        if [ $(cat screenlog.0 | wc -l ) -gt 200000 ]; then 
            echo ">> screenlog.0譌･蠢怜､・ｻｽ貂・炊螟・炊"
            rm -f screenlog.1
            mv screenlog.0 screenlog.1
        fi
    fi
    if $(in_cygwin); then
        werl -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} +P 204800 +K true -smp enable -hidden  -config sys.config -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name ${cfg['nodename']} -s main start -extra ${cfg['type']} &
    else
        start_file=${ROOT}/start.sh
        CMD="${ERL} -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} +P 204800 +K true -smp enable -hidden -config sys.config -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name ${cfg['nodename']} -s main start -extra ${cfg['type']}"
        cat > ${start_file} <<EOF
#!/bin/bash
cd ${ROOT}
ulimit -SHn 102400
${CMD}
EOF
        chmod +x ${start_file}
        screen -dmSL ${cfg['nodename']} -s ${start_file}
        echo ">> 闃らせ ${cfg['nodename']} 豁｣蝨ｨ蜷ｯ蜉ｨ荳ｭ・悟ｦよ棡諠ｳ隗ょｯ溷星蜉ｨ霑・ｨ玖ｯｷ菴ｿ逕ｨ莉･荳句多莉､霑幄｡悟星蜉ｨ:"
        echo ">> ./ctl.sh start && ./ctl.sh shell"
    fi
}

# 貂・炊session
fun_clean_sess(){
    rm -rf var/sess/*
    echo "-------------------------------------------"
    echo ">> php session貂・炊螳梧・"
}

# 蜈ｳ髣ｭ闃らせ
fun_stop(){
    if $(in_cygwin); then
        echo ">> cygwin荳倶ｸ肴髪謖∵ｭ､蜻ｽ莉､"
        exit
    fi
    ${ERL} -noshell -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} -hidden -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name stop_${cfg['nodename']} -setcookie ${cfg['cookie']} -eval "io:setopts([{encoding,unicode}])" -s main stop_from_shell -extra ${cfg['nodename']}
    fun_clean_sess
    echo "-------------------------------------------"
    num=0
    while(( $num<30 )); do
        if [ $(screen -ls | grep "${cfg['nodename']}" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ]; then 
            log=$(tail -n 10 ${ROOT}/screenlog.0)
            if [ $(echo $log | grep "type: temporary" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ] || [ $(echo $log | grep "Application inets exited with reason: stopped" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ] || [ $(echo $log | grep "Application main exited with reason: stopped" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ]; then
                pid=`ps aux | grep "\-name ${cfg['nodename']}"|grep erl|awk '{print $2}'` 
                if [ "$pid" != "" ]; then kill -9 $pid && echo "闃らせ[${cfg['nodename']}]蟾ｲ蜈ｳ髣ｭ・檎峩謗･kill謗芽ｿ帷ｨ・$log"; fi
                exit 0
            fi
            sleep 0.5
            let "num++"
        else
            echo ">> 闃らせ ${cfg['nodename']} 蜈ｳ髣ｭ螳梧・"
            exit 0
        fi
    done
    echo -e ">> 闃らせ ${cfg['nodename']} \e[91m譌豕募・髣ｭ闃らせ・瑚鰍轤ｹ霑伜惠豁｣蟶ｸ霑占｡御ｸｭ...\e[0;0m"
}

# 菴ｿ逕ｨremsh譁ｹ蠑剰ｿ帛・謗ｧ蛻ｶ蜿ｰ
fun_remsh(){
    n=remsh_$1_${cfg['nodename']}
    ${ERL} -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} -hidden -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name $n -setcookie ${cfg['cookie']} -remsh ${cfg['nodename']}
}

# 霑帛・隸･闃らせ逧・而蛻ｶ蜿ｰ
fun_shell(){
    if $(in_cygwin); then
        echo ">> cygwin荳倶ｸ肴髪謖∵ｭ､蜻ｽ莉､"
        exit
    fi
    screen -r "${cfg['nodename']}"
}

# 蛻ｷ譁ｰ逕ｨ謌ｷ蜷・
fun_set_db_user(){
    if [ "${cfg['db_user']}" != "root" ]; then
        echo "譖ｴ譁ｰ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎甜${cfg['db_name']}]逕ｨ謌ｷ[${cfg['db_user']}]譚・剞"
        sql="GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON ${cfg['db_name']}.* TO '${cfg['db_user']}'@'127.0.0.1' IDENTIFIED BY '${cfg['db_pass']}' WITH GRANT OPTION;"
        sql="$sql GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON ${cfg['db_name']}.* TO '${cfg['db_user']}'@'localhost' IDENTIFIED BY '${cfg['db_pass']}' WITH GRANT OPTION;"
        sql="$sql FLUSH PRIVILEGES;"
        mysql -uroot -p$(cat /data/save/mysql_root) -e "$sql"
    fi
}

# 螳芽｣・鰍轤ｹ
fun_install(){

    # 螟・炊讓｡譚ｿ
    tpls=( 'env_zone.cfg' 'vhost.conf' 'main.app' 'env.php' 'default.cfg.php' 'zone.sql' 'elog.config' 'emysql.app' 'lager.app' 'sys.config' 'goldrush.app' 'ranch.app' )
    fun_set_db_user
    fun_ssl
    for v in ${tpls[*]};do
        file="${CODE_PATH}/server/tpl/${v}"
        if [ ! -e $file ]; then
            echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]謇ｾ荳榊芦讓｡譚ｿ譁・ｻｶ: ${file}"
            exit 1
        fi
        # 譖ｿ謐｢讓｡譚ｿ蜿倬㍼
        lines=$(<"${file}")
        for k in ${!cfg[@]}; do
            lines=${lines//\{\{$k\}\}/${cfg[$k]}}
        done
        # 譖ｿ謐｢遨ｺ蜿倬㍼蟷ｶ逕滓・譁・ｻｶ
        echo "${lines}" | sed -e "s/{{.*}}//" > ${v}
    done
    # 螳芽｣・焚謐ｮ蠎・    mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -e"create database ${cfg['db_name']}"
    if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]蛻帛ｻｺ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎捺慮蜿醍函蠑ょｸｸ"
        exit 1
    fi
    mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -e"source ${ROOT}/zone.sql"
    if [ -f "${CODE_PATH}/server/tpl/card.sql" ]; then 
        mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -e"source ${CODE_PATH}/server/tpl/card.sql"
    fi
    if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]蟇ｼ蜈･謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎楢｡ｨ扈捺桷譌ｶ蜿醍函蠑ょｸｸ"
        exit 1
    fi
    # 螟・炊逶ｸ蜈ｳ譁・ｻｶ
    mkdir -p dets var/sess log/pack log_file role_face_photo/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']} ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/role_face_photo
    mv env_zone.cfg env.cfg
    ln -s ${CODE_PATH}/web/www .
    # ln -s ${CODE_PATH}/server/priv .
    fun_face_link
    rm -f zone.sql
    chmod -R 777 ${cfg['root']}/var
    echo ">> 豁｣蝨ｨ謇灘ｼ遶ｯ蜿｣:${cfg['port']}・悟ｦよ棡螟ｱ雍･蜿ｯ謇句勘謫堺ｽ懷ｮ梧・・梧裏髴驥肴眠螳芽｣・
    iptables -A RH-Firewall-1-INPUT -p tcp -m state --state NEW -m tcp --dport ${cfg['port']} -j ACCEPT && service iptables save && service iptables restart && sysctl -p
    rm -f sys_notice.sql
    echo ">> 豁｣蝨ｨ驟咲ｽｮweb譛榊苅蝎ｨ・悟ｦよ棡螟ｱ雍･蜿ｯ謇句勘謫堺ｽ懷ｮ梧・・梧裏髴驥肴眠螳芽｣・
    rm -f ${VHOST_PATH}/${cfg['host']}.conf && ln -s ${cfg['root']}/vhost.conf ${VHOST_PATH}/${cfg['host']}.conf
    /root/nginx_reload
    echo ">> 闃らせ螳芽｣・ｮ梧・"
}

# 蛻髯､闃らせ
fun_uninstall(){
    fun_check_screen
    role_num=$(mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -ss -e"select count(*) from role")
    if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]隶ｿ髣ｮ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎灘､ｱ雍･・梧裏豕戊執蜿冶ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥・
        exit 1
    fi
    if [ "$role_num" != "" ] && [ "$role_num" = "$1" ]; then
        mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -e"drop database ${cfg['db_name']}"
        if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
            echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]隶ｿ髣ｮ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎灘､ｱ雍･・梧裏豕募唖髯､謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎難ｼ悟ｦる怙扈ｧ扈ｭ隸ｷ謇句勘謫堺ｽ・
            exit 1
        fi
        echo ">> 豁｣蝨ｨ蜈ｳ髣ｭ遶ｯ蜿｣:${cfg['port']}"
        iptables -D RH-Firewall-1-INPUT -p tcp -m state --state NEW -m tcp --dport ${cfg['port']} -j ACCEPT && service iptables save && service iptables restart && sysctl -p
        rm -rf screenlog.* face replay replay_share dets priv log log_file var www env.cfg env.php default.cfg.php emysql.app main.app vhost.conf start.sh ${VHOST_PATH}/${cfg['host']}.conf ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/face/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']} ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/replay/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']}

        echo ">> 闃らせ${cfg['nodename']}蟾ｲ扈丞唖髯､"
    else
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]蛻髯､蜿よ焚荳ｭ蠢・｡ｻ蟶ｦ譛牙ｽ灘燕闃らせ逧・ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥擾ｼ瑚ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥丈ｸｺ[${role_num}]"
    fi
}

# 辜ｭ譖ｴ譁ｰ闃らせ
fun_update_cfg(){
    # 螟・炊讓｡譚ｿ
    tpls=( 'env_zone.cfg' 'vhost.conf' 'elog.config' 'main.app' 'env.php' 'default.cfg.php' )
    fun_set_db_user
    fun_ssl
    for v in ${tpls[*]};do
        file="${CODE_PATH}/server/tpl/${v}"
        # 譖ｿ謐｢讓｡譚ｿ蜿倬㍼
        lines=$(<"${file}")
        for k in ${!cfg[@]}; do
            lines=${lines//\{\{$k\}\}/${cfg[$k]}}
        done
        # 譖ｿ謐｢遨ｺ蜿倬㍼蟷ｶ逕滓・譁・ｻｶ
        echo "${lines}" | sed -e "s/{{.*}}//" > ${v}
    done
    rm -f env.cfg && mv env_zone.cfg env.cfg
    rm -f www && ln -s ${CODE_PATH}/web/www .
    mkdir -p ${cfg['root']}/role_face_photo/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']} ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/role_face_photo
    fun_face_link
    /root/nginx_reload
    if [ $(cat /etc/sysconfig/iptables | grep -e "--dport ${cfg['port']}" | wc -l ) -eq 0 ]; then 
        iptables -A RH-Firewall-1-INPUT -p tcp -m state --state NEW -m tcp --dport ${cfg['port']} -j ACCEPT && service iptables save && service iptables restart && sysctl -p
    fi
    echo ">> 謇譛蛾・鄂ｮ譁・ｻｶ譖ｴ譁ｰ螳梧・"
    if [ "$1" != "false" ] && [ $(screen -ls | grep "${cfg['nodename']}" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ]; then 
        fun_exec "sys_env:reload:[]"
    fi
}

# 蛻帛ｻｺ謇譛牙・譛榊ｹｳ蜿ｰ逶ｮ蠖・fun_create_combine(){
    dir=${cfg['root']}/$1
    # cfg['combine']='{<<"4399">>,1},{<<"4399">>,2},{<<"4399">>,3},{<<"4399">>,4},{<<"4399">>,5},{<<"4399">>,6},{<<"4399">>,7},{<<"4399">>,8},{<<"4399">>,9}'
    combinestr=${cfg['combine']}
    combinestr=${combinestr//\ /}
    combinestr=${combinestr//\{\<\<\"/}
    combinestr=${combinestr//\"\>\>\,/\_}
    combinestr=${combinestr//\}\,/\ }
    combinestr=( ${combinestr//\}/} )
    for k in ${combinestr[@]}; do 
        # echo ${dir}/${k}
        mkdir -p ${dir}/${k}
    done
}

# 譖ｴ譁ｰface體ｾ謗･
fun_face_link(){
    for dir in $(ls ${cfg['root']}/role_face_photo); do 
        if [ -d "${cfg['root']}/role_face_photo/${dir}" ] && [ "${dir}" != "." ]; then 
            rm -f ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/role_face_photo/${dir}
            ln -s ${cfg['root']}/role_face_photo/${dir} ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/role_face_photo/${dir}
        fi
    done
}

# 辜ｭ譖ｴ譁ｰ闃らせ
fun_hotswap(){
    noshell=""  # shell蜷主床螟夂ｺｿ遞狗Ο譖ｴ隕∵ｱゆｸｺnoshell
    if [ "$1" != "" ]; then 
        noshell=" -noshell"
    fi
    ${ERL}${noshell} -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} -hidden -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name hotswap_${cfg['nodename']} -setcookie ${cfg['cookie']} -eval "io:setopts([{encoding,unicode}])" -s main hotswap_from_shell -extra ${cfg['nodename']}
    echo ">> 辜ｭ譖ｴ譁ｰ螳梧・"
}

# 謇ｧ陦梧欠螳夊・譛ｬ 
fun_exec(){
    # 隕∵ｱゆｼ蜈･譬ｼ蠑丈ｸｺ Mod:Fun:Args(荳ｭ髣ｴ荳榊ｸｦ遨ｺ譬ｼ隨ｦ蜿ｷ)
    line=$1
    mod=$(echo $line | cut -d':' -f1)
    fun=$(echo $line | cut -d':' -f2)
    args=$(echo $line | cut -d':' -f3)
    args=${args//\#/:}
    if [[ $(echo $mod | grep "^[a-z0-9_]\{1,50\}$") = "" ]]; then 
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]讓｡蝮怜錐譬ｼ蠑丈ｸ肴ｭ｣蟶ｸ・・{mod}"
        exit 1
    fi
    if [[ $(echo $fun | grep "^[a-z0-9_]\{1,50\}$") = "" ]]; then 
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]譁ｹ豕募錐譬ｼ蠑丈ｸ肴ｭ｣蟶ｸ・・{fun}"
        exit 1
    fi
    if [[ $(echo $args | grep "^\[.*\]$") = "" ]]; then 
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]譁ｹ豕募盾謨ｰ譬ｼ蠑丈ｸ肴ｭ｣蟶ｸ・・{args}"
        exit 1
    fi
    echo ">> 蜃・､・鴬陦梧婿豕戊ｰ・畑 apply(${mod}, ${fun}, ${args})"
    ${ERL} -noshell -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} -hidden -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name exec_${cfg['nodename']} -setcookie ${cfg['cookie']} -eval "io:setopts([{encoding,unicode}]),io:format(\"return:~w~n\", [rpc:call('${cfg['nodename']}', ${mod}, ${fun}, ${args})])" -s c q
}

# 貂・｡｣謫堺ｽ・fun_clean(){
    fun_check_screen
    role_num=$(mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -ss -e"select count(*) from role")
    if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]隶ｿ髣ｮ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎灘､ｱ雍･・梧裏豕戊執蜿冶ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥・
        exit 1
    fi
    if [ "$role_num" != "" ] && [ "$role_num" = "$1" ]; then
        # 謗帝勁蛻苓｡ｨ・梧ｳｨ諢丞惠謗帝勁蛻苓｡ｨ螟也噪謇譛芽｡ｨ蟆・ｼ夊｢ｫ貂・ｩｺ
        exclude=( "mod_notice" "mod_holiday_son" "mod_holiday_total" "mod_white_ip" "sys_notice_board" "mod_white_acc" )
        tabs=( $(mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -ss -e"show tables") )
        for v in ${tabs[@]}; do
            flag=0
            for v1 in ${exclude[@]}; do
                if [ "$v" = "$v1" ]; then
                    flag=1
                fi
            done
            if [ $flag = 0 ]; then
                sql="${sql} truncate table ${v};"
            fi
        done
        mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -ss -e"${sql}"
        if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
            echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]隶ｿ髣ｮ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎灘､ｱ雍･"
            exit 1
        fi
        rm -f ./env.dets
        mv dets/env.dets ./
        rm -f dets/*
        mv ./env.dets dets/
        rm -rf var/sess
        rm -rf log
        rm -rf log_file/*
        rm -rf lager_log/*
        rm -rf combat_replay/*
        rm -f screenlog.*
        rm -rf face/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']}/*
        rm -rf replay/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']}/*
        rm -rf replay_share/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']}/*
        mkdir -p log/pack
        mkdir -p var/sess
        chmod -R 777 var/sess
        echo "" > ./srv_clear_flag.txt
        echo ">> 貂・｡｣謫堺ｽ懷ｮ梧・"
    else
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]貂・｡｣譌ｶ蜿よ焚荳ｭ蠢・｡ｻ蟶ｦ譛牙ｽ灘燕闃らせ逧・ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥擾ｼ瑚ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥丈ｸｺ[${role_num}]"
    fi
}

# 蛻､譁ｭ霑占｡瑚鰍轤ｹ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎捺弍蜷ｦ霑伜ｭ伜惠 扈ｴ謚､霎・勧蟾･蜈ｷ
fun_run_db(){
    if [ $(screen -ls | grep "${cfg['nodename']}" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ]; then 
        if [ $(mysql  -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -e "show databases like '${cfg['db_name']}'" | wc -l ) -eq 0 ]; then 
            echo "譛榊苅蝎ｨ闃らせ[${cfg['nodename']}]霑伜惠霑占｡御ｸｭ・梧焚謐ｮ蠎甜${cfg['db_name']}]荳榊ｭ伜惠莠・
            exit 1
        fi
    fi
}

fun_check_screen() {
    if [ $(screen -ls | grep "${cfg['nodename']}" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ]; then 
        echo "譛榊苅蝎ｨ闃らせ[${cfg['nodename']}]霑伜惠霑占｡御ｸｭ・御ｸ崎・霑幄｡瑚ｯ･謫堺ｽ・
        exit 1
    fi
}

# 謾ｶ髮・ｹｶ謇灘桁譌･蠢玲枚莉ｶ
fun_pack(){
    cd ${ROOT}/log/pack 
    # 螯よ棡逶ｮ蠖暮撼遨ｺ蛻呵ｿ幄｡梧遠蛹・    if [ $(ls -al | wc -l) -gt 3 ]; then
        tar zcf ${ROOT}/log/log_${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']}_$(date +"%y%m%d%H%M%S").tar.gz *.txt && rm -f *.txt
        exit $? 
    fi
    echo "豐｡譛画枚莉ｶ蜿ｯ謇灘桁"
    exit 1
}

##
fun_ssl(){
    if [ "${cfg['platform']}" = "9388" ]; then
        cfg['ssl']="listen 443 ssl;
    ssl on;
    ssl_certificate_key /usr/local/webserver/nginx/conf/wildcard_20131129.key;
    ssl_certificate /usr/local/webserver/nginx/conf/wildcard.pem;"
    fi
}

## 譽豬区弍蜷ｦ蝨ｨcygwin邇ｯ蠅・ｸｭ
in_cygwin(){
    os=$(uname)
    [[ "${os:0:3}" == "CYG" ]]; return $?
}

## 蜻ｽ莉､陦悟ｸｮ蜉ｩ
help(){
    echo "start             蜷ｯ蜉ｨ闃らせ"
    echo "stop              蜈ｳ髣ｭ闃らせ(蜷梧慮莨夊・蜉ｨ貂・炊php session)"
    echo "shell             霑帛・謗ｧ蛻ｶ蜿ｰ・御ｹ溷庄菴ｿ逕ｨscreen蜻ｽ莉､逶ｴ謗･霑帛・"
    echo "remsh             莉･remsh譁ｹ蠑剰ｿ帛・謗ｧ蛻ｶ蜿ｰ"
    echo "install           螳芽｣・鰍轤ｹ"
    echo "uninstall         蛻髯､闃らせ"
    echo "clean             貂・｡｣謫堺ｽ・
    echo "clean_sess        貂・炊php session・悟・髣ｭ譛榊苅蝎ｨ蜷朱怙貂・炊・御ｸ咲┯邇ｩ螳ｶ蛻ｷ譁ｰ蜷主庄閭ｽ霑帛・譛榊苅蝎ｨ"
    echo "update_cfg        譖ｴ譁ｰ謇譛蛾・鄂ｮ譁・ｻｶ"
    echo "hotswap           辜ｭ譖ｴ譁ｰ闃らせ"
    echo "exec              謇ｧ陦梧欠螳壼多莉､: Mod:Fun:Args"
    echo "pack              謾ｶ髮・ｹｶ謇灘桁譌･蠢玲枚莉ｶ"
    echo "log               譟･逵区而蛻ｶ蜿ｰ譌･蠢・
    echo "check             蛻､譁ｭ蛻・せ譏ｯ蜷ｦ霑伜惠霑占｡御ｸｭ"
    echo "ls                蛻怜・謇譛臥噪erl闃らせ"
    echo "run_db            譽譟･闃らせ譏ｯ蜷ｦ蝨ｨ霑占｡檎憾諤∽ｽ・焚謐ｮ蠎灘ｷｲ陲ｫ蛻髯､(扈ｴ謚､霎・勧蟾･蜈ｷ)"
}

# ------------------------------------------------------
# 謇ｧ陦悟・蜿｣
# ------------------------------------------------------
declare -A cfg
init
case $1 in
    start) fun_start;;
    stop)  fun_stop;;
    shell) fun_shell;;
    remsh) fun_remsh $2;;
    install) fun_install;;
    uninstall) fun_uninstall $2;;
    clean) fun_clean $2;;
    clean_sess) fun_clean_sess;;
    update_cfg) fun_update_cfg $2;;
    hotswap) fun_hotswap $2;;
    exec) fun_exec "${2}";;
    pack) fun_pack;;
    check) fun_check_screen;;
    log) less ./screenlog.0;;
    ls) screen -ls;;
    nodo) exit 0;;
    create_dir) fun_create_combine;;
    run_db) fun_run_db;;
    *)
        echo "譛ｪ遏･謖・ｻ､・瑚ｯｷ菴ｿ逕ｨ莉･荳区怏謨域欠莉､"
        echo "----------------------------------------------------------"
        help
        exit 1
        ;;
esac
exit 0

<?php
/**----------------------------------------------------+
 * PHP邇ｯ蠅・・鄂ｮ譁・ｻｶ
 * @author yeahoo2000@gmail.com
 +-----------------------------------------------------*/

// 邇ｯ蠅・・蟋句喧
define('DEBUG', false);
[REDACTED SENSITIVE LINE]

// 髞呵ｯｯ謚･蜻願ｮｾ鄂ｮ
if(DEBUG){ 
    error_reporting(E_ALL);
    ini_set('display_errors', 1);
}else{
    error_reporting(0);
    ini_set('display_errors', 0);
}

//邉ｻ扈溽岼蠖慕ｻ捺桷
define('ZONE_ROOT',             getenv('ROOT'));
define('VAR_DIR',               ZONE_ROOT.'/var');
define('WEB_DIR',               ZONE_ROOT.'/www');
define('LIB_DIR',               "/data/sszg_code//v200408/web");

require LIB_DIR.'/global.php';

// 蟷ｳ蜿ｰ譬・ｯ・define('PLATFORM',              "center");
// 貂ｸ謌丞玄蜿ｷ
define('ZONE_ID',               0);
// 隸ｭ險隶ｾ鄂ｮ
define('LANG',                  "zh-CN");
// 荳ｻ蟇・徴
define('SERVER_KEY',            "dsgsdgdgs15dbf0fc4d7b24fd");

// --------------------------------------------------------
// 驟咲ｽｮ

%% -*- erlang -*-
%% Thanks to Ulf Wiger at Ericcson for these comments:
%%
%% This file is identified via the erl command line option -config File.
%% Note that File should have no extension, e.g.
%% erl -config .../sys (if this file is called sys.config)
%%
%% In this file, you can redefine application environment variables.
%% This way, you don't have to modify the .app files of e.g. OTP applications.
[
    {lager, [
            {handlers, [
                    {lager_console_backend,[info,{lager_default_formatter, [date, " ", time, " [",severity,"] ", pid, module, ":", function, ":", line, " ", message, "\n"]}]},
                    {lager_file_backend, [{file, "lager_log/error.log"}, {level, error},{count,10}]},
                    {lager_file_backend, [{file, "lager_log/console.log"}, {level, info},{count,10}]}
                ]
            }
        ]
    }
].

===== FILE_BEGIN: /data/zone/sszg_center_0/vhost.conf =====
966 /data/zone/sszg_center_0/vhost.conf
server {
    listen       ;
    
    server_name  192.168.1.234:88: ;
    index index.html index.htm index.php;
    root  /data/zone/sszg_center_0/www;
    #autoindex on;
    charset utf-8;
    #expires off;
    access_log off;

    location ~.*\.(swf|xml|mp3|png|jpg|gif|data)$ {
        expires       max;
    }
    location ~.*\.(css|js|mx)$ {
        expires       96h;
    }
    location ~ /.svn/ {
        deny all;
    }
    location ~ .php { # 荳ｺ莠・髪謖｝athinfo・碁怙蟆・桁蜷ｫ.php逧ФRL莠､扈冉astcgi隗｣譫撰ｼ悟酔譌ｶ髴隶ｾ鄂ｮphp.ini荳ｭ逧・gi.fix_pathinfo=1
        expires off;
        #access_log off;
        access_log  /data/logs/192.168.1.234:88_.log json;

        include fastcgi.conf;
        fastcgi_pass   unix:/tmp/php-cgi-70.sock;
        fastcgi_index index.php;
        fastcgi_param ROOT /data/zone/sszg_center_0; # 霑吩ｸｪ邇ｯ蠅・序驥城怙莨蜈･・悟凄蛻冽eb邉ｻ扈滓裏豕墓ｭ｣蟶ｸ蟾･菴・        fastcgi_param PATH_INFO $fastcgi_script_name;
    }
}

#!/bin/bash

erl -kernel inet_dist_listen_min 40100 -kernel inet_dist_listen_max 44000 +P 204800 +K true -smp enable -hidden -config sys.config -pa /data/sszg_code//v200408/server/ebin -name sszg_local_1@127.0.0.1 -s main start -extra zone

#/bin/bash
# ---------------------------------------------------------
# 闃らせ邂｡逅・ｷ･蜈ｷ
# @author yeahoo2000@gmail.com
# ---------------------------------------------------------

DEBUG=1 # 蠑蜿第ｨ｡蠑剰ｮｾ鄂ｮ(0:蜈ｳ髣ｭ 1:蠑蜷ｯ)

# 蛻晏ｧ句喧
init(){
    cfg_init
    cfg_check

    # 闃らせ蟾･菴懃岼蠖・    ROOT=${cfg['root']}
    # 莉｣遐∝ｺ捺園螟・岼蠖包ｼ御ｾ・ /data/game.dev
    CODE_PATH=${cfg['code_path']}/${cfg['ver']}

    # nginx陌壽供荳ｻ譛ｺ驟咲ｽｮ蟄俶叛逶ｮ蠖・    if [ -d "/data/conf/nginx/vhost" ]; then
        VHOST_PATH="/data/conf/nginx/vhost"
    else
        VHOST_PATH="/usr/local/webserver/nginx/conf/vhost"
    fi
    if [ "${cfg['combine']}" = "" ]; then
        cfg['combine']='<<"local_1">>'
    fi
    # VHOST_PATH="/etc/nginx/sites-enabled"
    if [ "${cfg['combine']}" = "" ]; then
        cfg['combine']='<<"local_1">>'
    fi

    # erl遞句ｺ乗園蝨ｨ霍ｯ蠕・    ERL=erl
    if [ -f /opt/erlang/otp19.2/lib/erlang/bin/erl ]; then
        ERL=/opt/erlang/otp19.2/lib/erlang/bin/erl
    elif [ -f /usr/local/erlang19/bin/erl ]; then
        ERL=/usr/local/erlang19/bin/erl
    fi
    # erl闃らせ髣ｴ霑樊磁遶ｯ蜿｣闌・峩
    ERL_PORT_MIN=40100
    # erl闃らせ髣ｴ霑樊磁遶ｯ蜿｣闌・峩
    ERL_PORT_MAX=44000

    # 霑帛・闃らせ譬ｹ逶ｮ蠖・    cd ${ROOT} || exit 1
}

# 驟咲ｽｮ菫｡諱ｯ譽譟･
cfg_check(){
    if [ -e "${cfg['root']}" ]; then
        return
    else if [ "${DEBUG}" != "1" ]; then
            echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]閼壽悽譛ｪ霑幄｡梧ｭ｣遑ｮ驟咲ｽｮ・悟ｦよ棡蟶梧悍莉･蠑蜿第ｨ｡蠑丞ｷ･菴懆ｯｷ蟆・EBUG隶ｾ鄂ｮ荳ｺ'1'・悟ｹｶ蝪ｫ蜀吝・髞ｮ驟咲ｽｮ"
            exit 1
        fi
    fi
    # 闔ｷ蜿門ｽ灘燕閼壽悽譁・ｻｶ謇蝨ｨ霍ｯ蠕・ｼ御ｽ應ｸｺ譬ｹ逶ｮ蠖・    SOURCE="${BASH_SOURCE[0]}"
    while [ -h "$SOURCE" ]; do
[REDACTED SENSITIVE LINE]
        SOURCE="$(readlink "$SOURCE")"
        [[ $SOURCE != /* ]] && SOURCE="$DIR/$SOURCE"
    done
[REDACTED SENSITIVE LINE]

    # 隹・ｯ墓ｨ｡蠑丈ｸ狗噪鮟倩ｮ､驟咲ｽｮ・悟ｦよ怏蠢・ｦ∬ｯｷ謇句勘菫ｮ謾ｹ
    # cygwin荳矩怙菴ｿ逕ｨdos譬ｼ蠑乗燕閭ｽ豁｣蟶ｸ・悟ｦ・d:/game.dev/zone/mxfx_local_1
    cfg['root']="${root}"
    # cygwin荳矩怙菴ｿ逕ｨdos譬ｼ蠑乗燕閭ｽ豁｣蟶ｸ・悟ｦ・d:/game.dev
    cfg['code_path']="/data/game.dev"
    cfg['nodename']="s1@local.dev"
    cfg['platform']="local"
    cfg['zone_id']="0"
    cfg['type']="zone"
    cfg['host']="local.dev"
    cfg['port']="8000"
    cfg['cookie']="n2su1x83b16,2uw2"
    cfg['srv_key']="a2bi2Zc89n129BaE3N0"
    cfg['other_key']="a2bi2Zc89n129BaE3N0"
    cfg['game_name']="mxfx"
    cfg['zone_name']="譌ｶ遨ｺ逾槫沺蠑蜿第恪"
    cfg['lang']="zh_CN"
    cfg['fcm']="0"
    cfg['db_host']="127.0.0.1"
    cfg['db_port']="3306"
    cfg['db_name']="mxfx_local_1"
    cfg['db_user']="root"
    cfg['db_pass']="Android"
    cfg['db_conn_min']="10"
    cfg['db_conn_max']="30"
    cfg['ip']="127.0.0.1"
    cfg['open_time']="1384853886"
    cfg['website']="http://sksy.cc"

    # 譖ｿ謐｢遨ｺ蜿倬㍼
    for k in ${!cfg[@]}; do
        cfg[$k]=${cfg[$k]/\{\{*\}\}/}
    done
}

# 蜷ｯ蜉ｨ闃らせ
fun_start(){
    if [ ! -e dets ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]蠖灘燕逶ｮ蠖穂ｸ区悴螳芽｣・鰍轤ｹ・瑚ｯｷ蜈域鴬陦悟ｮ芽｣・桃菴・
        exit 1
    fi
    if [ $(screen -ls | grep "${cfg['nodename']}" | wc -l ) -le 0 ]; then  #蛛懈恪譖ｴ譁ｰ譌ｶ蟇ｹ譌･蠢怜★謳ｬ遘ｻ螟・炊
        if [ $(cat screenlog.0 | wc -l ) -gt 200000 ]; then 
            echo ">> screenlog.0譌･蠢怜､・ｻｽ貂・炊螟・炊"
            rm -f screenlog.1
            mv screenlog.0 screenlog.1
        fi
    fi
    if $(in_cygwin); then
        werl -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} +P 204800 +K true -smp enable -hidden  -config sys.config -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name ${cfg['nodename']} -s main start -extra ${cfg['type']} &
    else
        start_file=${ROOT}/start.sh
        CMD="${ERL} -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} +P 204800 +K true -smp enable -hidden -config sys.config -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name ${cfg['nodename']} -s main start -extra ${cfg['type']}"
        cat > ${start_file} <<EOF
#!/bin/bash
cd ${ROOT}
ulimit -SHn 102400
${CMD}
EOF
        chmod +x ${start_file}
        screen -dmSL ${cfg['nodename']} -s ${start_file}
        echo ">> 闃らせ ${cfg['nodename']} 豁｣蝨ｨ蜷ｯ蜉ｨ荳ｭ・悟ｦよ棡諠ｳ隗ょｯ溷星蜉ｨ霑・ｨ玖ｯｷ菴ｿ逕ｨ莉･荳句多莉､霑幄｡悟星蜉ｨ:"
        echo ">> ./ctl.sh start && ./ctl.sh shell"
    fi
}

# 貂・炊session
fun_clean_sess(){
    rm -rf var/sess/*
    echo "-------------------------------------------"
    echo ">> php session貂・炊螳梧・"
}

# 蜈ｳ髣ｭ闃らせ
fun_stop(){
    if $(in_cygwin); then
        echo ">> cygwin荳倶ｸ肴髪謖∵ｭ､蜻ｽ莉､"
        exit
    fi
    ${ERL} -noshell -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} -hidden -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name stop_${cfg['nodename']} -setcookie ${cfg['cookie']} -eval "io:setopts([{encoding,unicode}])" -s main stop_from_shell -extra ${cfg['nodename']}
    fun_clean_sess
    echo "-------------------------------------------"
    num=0
    while(( $num<30 )); do
        if [ $(screen -ls | grep "${cfg['nodename']}" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ]; then 
            log=$(tail -n 10 ${ROOT}/screenlog.0)
            if [ $(echo $log | grep "type: temporary" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ] || [ $(echo $log | grep "Application inets exited with reason: stopped" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ] || [ $(echo $log | grep "Application main exited with reason: stopped" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ]; then
                pid=`ps aux | grep "\-name ${cfg['nodename']}"|grep erl|awk '{print $2}'` 
                if [ "$pid" != "" ]; then kill -9 $pid && echo "闃らせ[${cfg['nodename']}]蟾ｲ蜈ｳ髣ｭ・檎峩謗･kill謗芽ｿ帷ｨ・$log"; fi
                exit 0
            fi
            sleep 0.5
            let "num++"
        else
            echo ">> 闃らせ ${cfg['nodename']} 蜈ｳ髣ｭ螳梧・"
            exit 0
        fi
    done
    echo -e ">> 闃らせ ${cfg['nodename']} \e[91m譌豕募・髣ｭ闃らせ・瑚鰍轤ｹ霑伜惠豁｣蟶ｸ霑占｡御ｸｭ...\e[0;0m"
}

# 菴ｿ逕ｨremsh譁ｹ蠑剰ｿ帛・謗ｧ蛻ｶ蜿ｰ
fun_remsh(){
    n=remsh_$1_${cfg['nodename']}
    ${ERL} -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} -hidden -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name $n -setcookie ${cfg['cookie']} -remsh ${cfg['nodename']}
}

# 霑帛・隸･闃らせ逧・而蛻ｶ蜿ｰ
fun_shell(){
    if $(in_cygwin); then
        echo ">> cygwin荳倶ｸ肴髪謖∵ｭ､蜻ｽ莉､"
        exit
    fi
    screen -r "${cfg['nodename']}"
}

# 蛻ｷ譁ｰ逕ｨ謌ｷ蜷・
fun_set_db_user(){
    if [ "${cfg['db_user']}" != "root" ]; then
        echo "譖ｴ譁ｰ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎甜${cfg['db_name']}]逕ｨ謌ｷ[${cfg['db_user']}]譚・剞"
        sql="GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON ${cfg['db_name']}.* TO '${cfg['db_user']}'@'127.0.0.1' IDENTIFIED BY '${cfg['db_pass']}' WITH GRANT OPTION;"
        sql="$sql GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON ${cfg['db_name']}.* TO '${cfg['db_user']}'@'localhost' IDENTIFIED BY '${cfg['db_pass']}' WITH GRANT OPTION;"
        sql="$sql FLUSH PRIVILEGES;"
        mysql -uroot -p$(cat /data/save/mysql_root) -e "$sql"
    fi
}

# 螳芽｣・鰍轤ｹ
fun_install(){
    if [ -e dets ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]蠖灘燕謨ｰ謐ｮ逶ｮ蠖暮撼遨ｺ・悟庄閭ｽ譏ｯ蟾ｲ扈乗鴬陦瑚ｿ・ｮ芽｣・ｿ・ｨ具ｼ瑚ｯｷ蛻髯､蜷朱㍾隸・
        exit 1
    fi
    # 螟・炊讓｡譚ｿ
    tpls=( 'env_zone.cfg' 'vhost.conf' 'main.app' 'env.php' 'default.cfg.php' 'zone.sql' 'elog.config' 'emysql.app' 'lager.app' 'sys.config' 'goldrush.app' 'ranch.app' )
    fun_set_db_user
    fun_ssl
    for v in ${tpls[*]};do
        file="${CODE_PATH}/server/tpl/${v}"
        if [ ! -e $file ]; then
            echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]謇ｾ荳榊芦讓｡譚ｿ譁・ｻｶ: ${file}"
            exit 1
        fi
        # 譖ｿ謐｢讓｡譚ｿ蜿倬㍼
        lines=$(<"${file}")
        for k in ${!cfg[@]}; do
            lines=${lines//\{\{$k\}\}/${cfg[$k]}}
        done
        # 譖ｿ謐｢遨ｺ蜿倬㍼蟷ｶ逕滓・譁・ｻｶ
        echo "${lines}" | sed -e "s/{{.*}}//" > ${v}
    done
    # 螳芽｣・焚謐ｮ蠎・    mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -e"create database ${cfg['db_name']}"
    if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]蛻帛ｻｺ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎捺慮蜿醍函蠑ょｸｸ"
        exit 1
    fi
    mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -e"source ${ROOT}/zone.sql"
    if [ -f "${CODE_PATH}/server/tpl/card.sql" ]; then 
        mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -e"source ${CODE_PATH}/server/tpl/card.sql"
    fi
    if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]蟇ｼ蜈･謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎楢｡ｨ扈捺桷譌ｶ蜿醍函蠑ょｸｸ"
        exit 1
    fi
    # 螟・炊逶ｸ蜈ｳ譁・ｻｶ
    mkdir -p dets var/sess log/pack log_file role_face_photo/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']} ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/role_face_photo
    mv env_zone.cfg env.cfg
    ln -s ${CODE_PATH}/web/www .
    # ln -s ${CODE_PATH}/server/priv .
    fun_face_link
    rm -f zone.sql
    chmod -R 777 ${cfg['root']}/var
    echo ">> 豁｣蝨ｨ謇灘ｼ遶ｯ蜿｣:${cfg['port']}・悟ｦよ棡螟ｱ雍･蜿ｯ謇句勘謫堺ｽ懷ｮ梧・・梧裏髴驥肴眠螳芽｣・
    iptables -A RH-Firewall-1-INPUT -p tcp -m state --state NEW -m tcp --dport ${cfg['port']} -j ACCEPT && service iptables save && service iptables restart && sysctl -p
    rm -f sys_notice.sql
    echo ">> 豁｣蝨ｨ驟咲ｽｮweb譛榊苅蝎ｨ・悟ｦよ棡螟ｱ雍･蜿ｯ謇句勘謫堺ｽ懷ｮ梧・・梧裏髴驥肴眠螳芽｣・
    rm -f ${VHOST_PATH}/${cfg['host']}.conf && ln -s ${cfg['root']}/vhost.conf ${VHOST_PATH}/${cfg['host']}.conf
    /root/nginx_reload
    echo ">> 闃らせ螳芽｣・ｮ梧・"
}

# 蛻髯､闃らせ
fun_uninstall(){
    fun_check_screen
    role_num=$(mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -ss -e"select count(*) from role")
    if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]隶ｿ髣ｮ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎灘､ｱ雍･・梧裏豕戊執蜿冶ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥・
        exit 1
    fi
    if [ "$role_num" != "" ] && [ "$role_num" = "$1" ]; then
        mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -e"drop database ${cfg['db_name']}"
        if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
            echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]隶ｿ髣ｮ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎灘､ｱ雍･・梧裏豕募唖髯､謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎難ｼ悟ｦる怙扈ｧ扈ｭ隸ｷ謇句勘謫堺ｽ・
            exit 1
        fi
        echo ">> 豁｣蝨ｨ蜈ｳ髣ｭ遶ｯ蜿｣:${cfg['port']}"
        iptables -D RH-Firewall-1-INPUT -p tcp -m state --state NEW -m tcp --dport ${cfg['port']} -j ACCEPT && service iptables save && service iptables restart && sysctl -p
        rm -rf screenlog.* face replay replay_share dets priv log log_file var www env.cfg env.php default.cfg.php emysql.app main.app vhost.conf start.sh ${VHOST_PATH}/${cfg['host']}.conf ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/face/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']} ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/replay/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']}

        echo ">> 闃らせ${cfg['nodename']}蟾ｲ扈丞唖髯､"
    else
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]蛻髯､蜿よ焚荳ｭ蠢・｡ｻ蟶ｦ譛牙ｽ灘燕闃らせ逧・ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥擾ｼ瑚ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥丈ｸｺ[${role_num}]"
    fi
}

# 辜ｭ譖ｴ譁ｰ闃らせ
fun_update_cfg(){
    # 螟・炊讓｡譚ｿ
    tpls=( 'env_zone.cfg' 'vhost.conf' 'elog.config' 'main.app' 'env.php' 'default.cfg.php' )
    fun_set_db_user
    fun_ssl
    for v in ${tpls[*]};do
        file="${CODE_PATH}/server/tpl/${v}"
        # 譖ｿ謐｢讓｡譚ｿ蜿倬㍼
        lines=$(<"${file}")
        for k in ${!cfg[@]}; do
            lines=${lines//\{\{$k\}\}/${cfg[$k]}}
        done
        # 譖ｿ謐｢遨ｺ蜿倬㍼蟷ｶ逕滓・譁・ｻｶ
        echo "${lines}" | sed -e "s/{{.*}}//" > ${v}
    done
    rm -f env.cfg && mv env_zone.cfg env.cfg
    rm -f www && ln -s ${CODE_PATH}/web/www .
    mkdir -p ${cfg['root']}/role_face_photo/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']} ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/role_face_photo
    fun_face_link
    /root/nginx_reload
    if [ $(cat /etc/sysconfig/iptables | grep -e "--dport ${cfg['port']}" | wc -l ) -eq 0 ]; then 
        iptables -A RH-Firewall-1-INPUT -p tcp -m state --state NEW -m tcp --dport ${cfg['port']} -j ACCEPT && service iptables save && service iptables restart && sysctl -p
    fi
    echo ">> 謇譛蛾・鄂ｮ譁・ｻｶ譖ｴ譁ｰ螳梧・"
    if [ "$1" != "false" ] && [ $(screen -ls | grep "${cfg['nodename']}" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ]; then 
        fun_exec "sys_env:reload:[]"
    fi
}

# 蛻帛ｻｺ謇譛牙・譛榊ｹｳ蜿ｰ逶ｮ蠖・fun_create_combine(){
    dir=${cfg['root']}/$1
    # cfg['combine']='{<<"4399">>,1},{<<"4399">>,2},{<<"4399">>,3},{<<"4399">>,4},{<<"4399">>,5},{<<"4399">>,6},{<<"4399">>,7},{<<"4399">>,8},{<<"4399">>,9}'
    combinestr=${cfg['combine']}
    combinestr=${combinestr//\ /}
    combinestr=${combinestr//\{\<\<\"/}
    combinestr=${combinestr//\"\>\>\,/\_}
    combinestr=${combinestr//\}\,/\ }
    combinestr=( ${combinestr//\}/} )
    for k in ${combinestr[@]}; do 
        # echo ${dir}/${k}
        mkdir -p ${dir}/${k}
    done
}

# 譖ｴ譁ｰface體ｾ謗･
fun_face_link(){
    for dir in $(ls ${cfg['root']}/role_face_photo); do 
        if [ -d "${cfg['root']}/role_face_photo/${dir}" ] && [ "${dir}" != "." ]; then 
            rm -f ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/role_face_photo/${dir}
            ln -s ${cfg['root']}/role_face_photo/${dir} ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/role_face_photo/${dir}
        fi
    done
}

# 辜ｭ譖ｴ譁ｰ闃らせ
fun_hotswap(){
    noshell=""  # shell蜷主床螟夂ｺｿ遞狗Ο譖ｴ隕∵ｱゆｸｺnoshell
    if [ "$1" != "" ]; then 
        noshell=" -noshell"
    fi
    ${ERL}${noshell} -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} -hidden -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name hotswap_${cfg['nodename']} -setcookie ${cfg['cookie']} -eval "io:setopts([{encoding,unicode}])" -s main hotswap_from_shell -extra ${cfg['nodename']}
    echo ">> 辜ｭ譖ｴ譁ｰ螳梧・"
}

# 謇ｧ陦梧欠螳夊・譛ｬ 
fun_exec(){
    # 隕∵ｱゆｼ蜈･譬ｼ蠑丈ｸｺ Mod:Fun:Args(荳ｭ髣ｴ荳榊ｸｦ遨ｺ譬ｼ隨ｦ蜿ｷ)
    line=$1
    mod=$(echo $line | cut -d':' -f1)
    fun=$(echo $line | cut -d':' -f2)
    args=$(echo $line | cut -d':' -f3)
    args=${args//\#/:}
    if [[ $(echo $mod | grep "^[a-z0-9_]\{1,50\}$") = "" ]]; then 
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]讓｡蝮怜錐譬ｼ蠑丈ｸ肴ｭ｣蟶ｸ・・{mod}"
        exit 1
    fi
    if [[ $(echo $fun | grep "^[a-z0-9_]\{1,50\}$") = "" ]]; then 
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]譁ｹ豕募錐譬ｼ蠑丈ｸ肴ｭ｣蟶ｸ・・{fun}"
        exit 1
    fi
    if [[ $(echo $args | grep "^\[.*\]$") = "" ]]; then 
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]譁ｹ豕募盾謨ｰ譬ｼ蠑丈ｸ肴ｭ｣蟶ｸ・・{args}"
        exit 1
    fi
    echo ">> 蜃・､・鴬陦梧婿豕戊ｰ・畑 apply(${mod}, ${fun}, ${args})"
    ${ERL} -noshell -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} -hidden -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name exec_${cfg['nodename']} -setcookie ${cfg['cookie']} -eval "io:setopts([{encoding,unicode}]),io:format(\"return:~w~n\", [rpc:call('${cfg['nodename']}', ${mod}, ${fun}, ${args})])" -s c q
}

# 貂・｡｣謫堺ｽ・fun_clean(){
    fun_check_screen
    role_num=$(mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -ss -e"select count(*) from role")
    if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]隶ｿ髣ｮ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎灘､ｱ雍･・梧裏豕戊執蜿冶ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥・
        exit 1
    fi
    if [ "$role_num" != "" ] && [ "$role_num" = "$1" ]; then
        # 謗帝勁蛻苓｡ｨ・梧ｳｨ諢丞惠謗帝勁蛻苓｡ｨ螟也噪謇譛芽｡ｨ蟆・ｼ夊｢ｫ貂・ｩｺ
        exclude=( "mod_notice" "mod_holiday_son" "mod_holiday_total" "mod_white_ip" "sys_notice_board" "mod_white_acc" )
        tabs=( $(mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -ss -e"show tables") )
        for v in ${tabs[@]}; do
            flag=0
            for v1 in ${exclude[@]}; do
                if [ "$v" = "$v1" ]; then
                    flag=1
                fi
            done
            if [ $flag = 0 ]; then
                sql="${sql} truncate table ${v};"
            fi
        done
        mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -ss -e"${sql}"
        if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
            echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]隶ｿ髣ｮ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎灘､ｱ雍･"
            exit 1
        fi
        rm -f ./env.dets
        mv dets/env.dets ./
        rm -f dets/*
        mv ./env.dets dets/
        rm -rf var/sess
        rm -rf log
        rm -rf log_file/*
        rm -rf lager_log/*
        rm -rf combat_replay/*
        rm -f screenlog.*
        rm -rf face/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']}/*
        rm -rf replay/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']}/*
        rm -rf replay_share/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']}/*
        mkdir -p log/pack
        mkdir -p var/sess
        chmod -R 777 var/sess
        echo "" > ./srv_clear_flag.txt
        echo ">> 貂・｡｣謫堺ｽ懷ｮ梧・"
    else
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]貂・｡｣譌ｶ蜿よ焚荳ｭ蠢・｡ｻ蟶ｦ譛牙ｽ灘燕闃らせ逧・ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥擾ｼ瑚ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥丈ｸｺ[${role_num}]"
    fi
}

# 蛻､譁ｭ霑占｡瑚鰍轤ｹ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎捺弍蜷ｦ霑伜ｭ伜惠 扈ｴ謚､霎・勧蟾･蜈ｷ
fun_run_db(){
    if [ $(screen -ls | grep "${cfg['nodename']}" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ]; then 
        if [ $(mysql  -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -e "show databases like '${cfg['db_name']}'" | wc -l ) -eq 0 ]; then 
            echo "譛榊苅蝎ｨ闃らせ[${cfg['nodename']}]霑伜惠霑占｡御ｸｭ・梧焚謐ｮ蠎甜${cfg['db_name']}]荳榊ｭ伜惠莠・
            exit 1
        fi
    fi
}

fun_check_screen() {
    if [ $(screen -ls | grep "${cfg['nodename']}" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ]; then 
        echo "譛榊苅蝎ｨ闃らせ[${cfg['nodename']}]霑伜惠霑占｡御ｸｭ・御ｸ崎・霑幄｡瑚ｯ･謫堺ｽ・
        exit 1
    fi
}

# 謾ｶ髮・ｹｶ謇灘桁譌･蠢玲枚莉ｶ
fun_pack(){
    cd ${ROOT}/log/pack 
    # 螯よ棡逶ｮ蠖暮撼遨ｺ蛻呵ｿ幄｡梧遠蛹・    if [ $(ls -al | wc -l) -gt 3 ]; then
        tar zcf ${ROOT}/log/log_${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']}_$(date +"%y%m%d%H%M%S").tar.gz *.txt && rm -f *.txt
        exit $? 
    fi
    echo "豐｡譛画枚莉ｶ蜿ｯ謇灘桁"
    exit 1
}

##
fun_ssl(){
    if [ "${cfg['platform']}" = "9388" ]; then
        cfg['ssl']="listen 443 ssl;
    ssl on;
    ssl_certificate_key /usr/local/webserver/nginx/conf/wildcard_20131129.key;
    ssl_certificate /usr/local/webserver/nginx/conf/wildcard.pem;"
    fi
}

## 譽豬区弍蜷ｦ蝨ｨcygwin邇ｯ蠅・ｸｭ
in_cygwin(){
    os=$(uname)
    [[ "${os:0:3}" == "CYG" ]]; return $?
}

## 蜻ｽ莉､陦悟ｸｮ蜉ｩ
help(){
    echo "start             蜷ｯ蜉ｨ闃らせ"
    echo "stop              蜈ｳ髣ｭ闃らせ(蜷梧慮莨夊・蜉ｨ貂・炊php session)"
    echo "shell             霑帛・謗ｧ蛻ｶ蜿ｰ・御ｹ溷庄菴ｿ逕ｨscreen蜻ｽ莉､逶ｴ謗･霑帛・"
    echo "remsh             莉･remsh譁ｹ蠑剰ｿ帛・謗ｧ蛻ｶ蜿ｰ"
    echo "install           螳芽｣・鰍轤ｹ"
    echo "uninstall         蛻髯､闃らせ"
    echo "clean             貂・｡｣謫堺ｽ・
    echo "clean_sess        貂・炊php session・悟・髣ｭ譛榊苅蝎ｨ蜷朱怙貂・炊・御ｸ咲┯邇ｩ螳ｶ蛻ｷ譁ｰ蜷主庄閭ｽ霑帛・譛榊苅蝎ｨ"
    echo "update_cfg        譖ｴ譁ｰ謇譛蛾・鄂ｮ譁・ｻｶ"
    echo "hotswap           辜ｭ譖ｴ譁ｰ闃らせ"
    echo "exec              謇ｧ陦梧欠螳壼多莉､: Mod:Fun:Args"
    echo "pack              謾ｶ髮・ｹｶ謇灘桁譌･蠢玲枚莉ｶ"
    echo "log               譟･逵区而蛻ｶ蜿ｰ譌･蠢・
    echo "check             蛻､譁ｭ蛻・せ譏ｯ蜷ｦ霑伜惠霑占｡御ｸｭ"
    echo "ls                蛻怜・謇譛臥噪erl闃らせ"
    echo "run_db            譽譟･闃らせ譏ｯ蜷ｦ蝨ｨ霑占｡檎憾諤∽ｽ・焚謐ｮ蠎灘ｷｲ陲ｫ蛻髯､(扈ｴ謚､霎・勧蟾･蜈ｷ)"
}

# ------------------------------------------------------
# 謇ｧ陦悟・蜿｣
# ------------------------------------------------------
declare -A cfg
init
case $1 in
    start) fun_start;;
    stop)  fun_stop;;
    shell) fun_shell;;
    remsh) fun_remsh $2;;
    install) fun_install;;
    uninstall) fun_uninstall $2;;
    clean) fun_clean $2;;
    clean_sess) fun_clean_sess;;
    update_cfg) fun_update_cfg $2;;
    hotswap) fun_hotswap $2;;
    exec) fun_exec "${2}";;
    pack) fun_pack;;
    check) fun_check_screen;;
    log) less ./screenlog.0;;
    ls) screen -ls;;
    nodo) exit 0;;
    create_dir) fun_create_combine;;
    run_db) fun_run_db;;
    *)
        echo "譛ｪ遏･謖・ｻ､・瑚ｯｷ菴ｿ逕ｨ莉･荳区怏謨域欠莉､"
        echo "----------------------------------------------------------"
        help
        exit 1
        ;;
esac
exit 0

964 /data/zone/sszg_symlf_1/vhost.conf
server {
    listen       ;
    
    server_name  192.168.1.234:81: ;
    index index.html index.htm index.php;
    root  /data/zone/sszg_symlf_1/www;
    #autoindex on;
    charset utf-8;
    #expires off;
    access_log off;

    location ~.*\.(swf|xml|mp3|png|jpg|gif|data)$ {
        expires       max;
    }
    location ~.*\.(css|js|mx)$ {
        expires       96h;
    }
    location ~ /.svn/ {
        deny all;
    }
    location ~ .php { # 荳ｺ莠・髪謖｝athinfo・碁怙蟆・桁蜷ｫ.php逧ФRL莠､扈冉astcgi隗｣譫撰ｼ悟酔譌ｶ髴隶ｾ鄂ｮphp.ini荳ｭ逧・gi.fix_pathinfo=1
        expires off;
        #access_log off;
        access_log  /data/logs/192.168.1.234:81_.log json;

        include fastcgi.conf;
        fastcgi_pass   unix:/tmp/php-cgi-70.sock;
        fastcgi_index index.php;
        fastcgi_param ROOT /data/zone/sszg_symlf_1; # 霑吩ｸｪ邇ｯ蠅・序驥城怙莨蜈･・悟凄蛻冽eb邉ｻ扈滓裏豕墓ｭ｣蟶ｸ蟾･菴・        fastcgi_param PATH_INFO $fastcgi_script_name;
    }
}

#/bin/bash
# ---------------------------------------------------------
# 闃らせ邂｡逅・ｷ･蜈ｷ
# @author yeahoo2000@gmail.com
# ---------------------------------------------------------

DEBUG=1 # 蠑蜿第ｨ｡蠑剰ｮｾ鄂ｮ(0:蜈ｳ髣ｭ 1:蠑蜷ｯ)

# 蛻晏ｧ句喧
init(){
    cfg_init
    cfg_check

    # 闃らせ蟾･菴懃岼蠖・    ROOT=${cfg['root']}
    # 莉｣遐∝ｺ捺園螟・岼蠖包ｼ御ｾ・ /data/game.dev
    CODE_PATH=${cfg['code_path']}/${cfg['ver']}

    # nginx陌壽供荳ｻ譛ｺ驟咲ｽｮ蟄俶叛逶ｮ蠖・    if [ -d "/data/conf/nginx/vhost" ]; then
        VHOST_PATH="/data/conf/nginx/vhost"
    else
        VHOST_PATH="/usr/local/webserver/nginx/conf/vhost"
    fi
    if [ "${cfg['combine']}" = "" ]; then
        cfg['combine']='<<"local_2">>'
    fi
    # VHOST_PATH="/etc/nginx/sites-enabled"
    if [ "${cfg['combine']}" = "" ]; then
        cfg['combine']='<<"local_2">>'
    fi

    # erl遞句ｺ乗園蝨ｨ霍ｯ蠕・    ERL=erl
    if [ -f /opt/erlang/otp19.2/lib/erlang/bin/erl ]; then
        ERL=/opt/erlang/otp19.2/lib/erlang/bin/erl
    elif [ -f /usr/local/erlang19/bin/erl ]; then
        ERL=/usr/local/erlang19/bin/erl
    fi
    # erl闃らせ髣ｴ霑樊磁遶ｯ蜿｣闌・峩
    ERL_PORT_MIN=40100
    # erl闃らせ髣ｴ霑樊磁遶ｯ蜿｣闌・峩
    ERL_PORT_MAX=44000

    # 霑帛・闃らせ譬ｹ逶ｮ蠖・    cd ${ROOT} || exit 1
}

# 驟咲ｽｮ菫｡諱ｯ譽譟･
cfg_check(){
    if [ -e "${cfg['root']}" ]; then
        return
    else if [ "${DEBUG}" != "1" ]; then
            echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]閼壽悽譛ｪ霑幄｡梧ｭ｣遑ｮ驟咲ｽｮ・悟ｦよ棡蟶梧悍莉･蠑蜿第ｨ｡蠑丞ｷ･菴懆ｯｷ蟆・EBUG隶ｾ鄂ｮ荳ｺ'1'・悟ｹｶ蝪ｫ蜀吝・髞ｮ驟咲ｽｮ"
            exit 1
        fi
    fi
    # 闔ｷ蜿門ｽ灘燕閼壽悽譁・ｻｶ謇蝨ｨ霍ｯ蠕・ｼ御ｽ應ｸｺ譬ｹ逶ｮ蠖・    SOURCE="${BASH_SOURCE[0]}"
    while [ -h "$SOURCE" ]; do
[REDACTED SENSITIVE LINE]
        SOURCE="$(readlink "$SOURCE")"
        [[ $SOURCE != /* ]] && SOURCE="$DIR/$SOURCE"
    done
[REDACTED SENSITIVE LINE]

    # 隹・ｯ墓ｨ｡蠑丈ｸ狗噪鮟倩ｮ､驟咲ｽｮ・悟ｦよ怏蠢・ｦ∬ｯｷ謇句勘菫ｮ謾ｹ
    # cygwin荳矩怙菴ｿ逕ｨdos譬ｼ蠑乗燕閭ｽ豁｣蟶ｸ・悟ｦ・d:/game.dev/zone/mxfx_local_1
    cfg['root']="${root}"
    # cygwin荳矩怙菴ｿ逕ｨdos譬ｼ蠑乗燕閭ｽ豁｣蟶ｸ・悟ｦ・d:/game.dev
    cfg['code_path']="/data/game.dev"
    cfg['nodename']="s1@local.dev"
    cfg['platform']="local"
    cfg['zone_id']="0"
    cfg['type']="zone"
    cfg['host']="local.dev"
    cfg['port']="8000"
    cfg['cookie']="n2su1x83b16,2uw2"
    cfg['srv_key']="a2bi2Zc89n129BaE3N0"
    cfg['other_key']="a2bi2Zc89n129BaE3N0"
    cfg['game_name']="mxfx"
    cfg['zone_name']="譌ｶ遨ｺ逾槫沺蠑蜿第恪"
    cfg['lang']="zh_CN"
    cfg['fcm']="0"
    cfg['db_host']="127.0.0.1"
    cfg['db_port']="3306"
    cfg['db_name']="mxfx_local_1"
    cfg['db_user']="root"
    cfg['db_pass']="Android"
    cfg['db_conn_min']="10"
    cfg['db_conn_max']="30"
    cfg['ip']="127.0.0.1"
    cfg['open_time']="1384853886"
    cfg['website']="http://sksy.cc"

    # 譖ｿ謐｢遨ｺ蜿倬㍼
    for k in ${!cfg[@]}; do
        cfg[$k]=${cfg[$k]/\{\{*\}\}/}
    done
}

# 驟咲ｽｮ菫｡諱ｯ蛻晏ｧ句喧
cfg_init(){

    # 蟷ｳ蜿ｰ譬・ｯ・    cfg['platform']="local"

    cfg['db_host']="127.0.0.1"

    # 謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎灘ｯ・・    cfg['db_pass']="Android"
    # 謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎捺怙蟆剰ｿ樊磁謨ｰ

    # 蠖灘燕迚域悽(豁､迚域悽蜿ｷ逕ｨ莠取・ｯ・ｽ灘燕迚域悽・檎罰螳芽｣・ｷ･蜈ｷ閾ｪ蜉ｨ蝪ｫ蜀・
    cfg['ver']="v200408"

    cfg['lang']="zh-CN"

    cfg['is_maintain']="0"

    cfg['flume_host_group']="1"
}

# 蜷ｯ蜉ｨ闃らせ
fun_start(){
    if [ ! -e dets ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]蠖灘燕逶ｮ蠖穂ｸ区悴螳芽｣・鰍轤ｹ・瑚ｯｷ蜈域鴬陦悟ｮ芽｣・桃菴・
        exit 1
    fi
    if [ $(screen -ls | grep "${cfg['nodename']}" | wc -l ) -le 0 ]; then  #蛛懈恪譖ｴ譁ｰ譌ｶ蟇ｹ譌･蠢怜★謳ｬ遘ｻ螟・炊
        if [ $(cat screenlog.0 | wc -l ) -gt 200000 ]; then 
            echo ">> screenlog.0譌･蠢怜､・ｻｽ貂・炊螟・炊"
            rm -f screenlog.1
            mv screenlog.0 screenlog.1
        fi
    fi
    if $(in_cygwin); then
        werl -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} +P 204800 +K true -smp enable -hidden  -config sys.config -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name ${cfg['nodename']} -s main start -extra ${cfg['type']} &
    else
        start_file=${ROOT}/start.sh
        CMD="${ERL} -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} +P 204800 +K true -smp enable -hidden -config sys.config -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name ${cfg['nodename']} -s main start -extra ${cfg['type']}"
        cat > ${start_file} <<EOF
#!/bin/bash
cd ${ROOT}
ulimit -SHn 102400
${CMD}
EOF
        chmod +x ${start_file}
        screen -dmSL ${cfg['nodename']} -s ${start_file}
        echo ">> 闃らせ ${cfg['nodename']} 豁｣蝨ｨ蜷ｯ蜉ｨ荳ｭ・悟ｦよ棡諠ｳ隗ょｯ溷星蜉ｨ霑・ｨ玖ｯｷ菴ｿ逕ｨ莉･荳句多莉､霑幄｡悟星蜉ｨ:"
        echo ">> ./ctl.sh start && ./ctl.sh shell"
    fi
}

# 貂・炊session
fun_clean_sess(){
    rm -rf var/sess/*
    echo "-------------------------------------------"
    echo ">> php session貂・炊螳梧・"
}

# 蜈ｳ髣ｭ闃らせ
fun_stop(){
    if $(in_cygwin); then
        echo ">> cygwin荳倶ｸ肴髪謖∵ｭ､蜻ｽ莉､"
        exit
    fi
    ${ERL} -noshell -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} -hidden -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name stop_${cfg['nodename']} -setcookie ${cfg['cookie']} -eval "io:setopts([{encoding,unicode}])" -s main stop_from_shell -extra ${cfg['nodename']}
    fun_clean_sess
    echo "-------------------------------------------"
    num=0
    while(( $num<30 )); do
        if [ $(screen -ls | grep "${cfg['nodename']}" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ]; then 
            log=$(tail -n 10 ${ROOT}/screenlog.0)
            if [ $(echo $log | grep "type: temporary" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ] || [ $(echo $log | grep "Application inets exited with reason: stopped" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ] || [ $(echo $log | grep "Application main exited with reason: stopped" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ]; then
                pid=`ps aux | grep "\-name ${cfg['nodename']}"|grep erl|awk '{print $2}'` 
                if [ "$pid" != "" ]; then kill -9 $pid && echo "闃らせ[${cfg['nodename']}]蟾ｲ蜈ｳ髣ｭ・檎峩謗･kill謗芽ｿ帷ｨ・$log"; fi
                exit 0
            fi
            sleep 0.5
            let "num++"
        else
            echo ">> 闃らせ ${cfg['nodename']} 蜈ｳ髣ｭ螳梧・"
            exit 0
        fi
    done
    echo -e ">> 闃らせ ${cfg['nodename']} \e[91m譌豕募・髣ｭ闃らせ・瑚鰍轤ｹ霑伜惠豁｣蟶ｸ霑占｡御ｸｭ...\e[0;0m"
}

# 菴ｿ逕ｨremsh譁ｹ蠑剰ｿ帛・謗ｧ蛻ｶ蜿ｰ
fun_remsh(){
    n=remsh_$1_${cfg['nodename']}
    ${ERL} -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} -hidden -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name $n -setcookie ${cfg['cookie']} -remsh ${cfg['nodename']}
}

# 霑帛・隸･闃らせ逧・而蛻ｶ蜿ｰ
fun_shell(){
    if $(in_cygwin); then
        echo ">> cygwin荳倶ｸ肴髪謖∵ｭ､蜻ｽ莉､"
        exit
    fi
    screen -r "${cfg['nodename']}"
}

# 蛻ｷ譁ｰ逕ｨ謌ｷ蜷・
fun_set_db_user(){
    if [ "${cfg['db_user']}" != "root" ]; then
        echo "譖ｴ譁ｰ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎甜${cfg['db_name']}]逕ｨ謌ｷ[${cfg['db_user']}]譚・剞"
        sql="GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON ${cfg['db_name']}.* TO '${cfg['db_user']}'@'127.0.0.1' IDENTIFIED BY '${cfg['db_pass']}' WITH GRANT OPTION;"
        sql="$sql GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON ${cfg['db_name']}.* TO '${cfg['db_user']}'@'localhost' IDENTIFIED BY '${cfg['db_pass']}' WITH GRANT OPTION;"
        sql="$sql FLUSH PRIVILEGES;"
        mysql -uroot -p$(cat /data/save/mysql_root) -e "$sql"
    fi
}

# 螳芽｣・鰍轤ｹ
fun_install(){
    if [ -e dets ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]蠖灘燕謨ｰ謐ｮ逶ｮ蠖暮撼遨ｺ・悟庄閭ｽ譏ｯ蟾ｲ扈乗鴬陦瑚ｿ・ｮ芽｣・ｿ・ｨ具ｼ瑚ｯｷ蛻髯､蜷朱㍾隸・
        exit 1
    fi
    # 螟・炊讓｡譚ｿ
    tpls=( 'env_zone.cfg' 'vhost.conf' 'main.app' 'env.php' 'default.cfg.php' 'zone.sql' 'elog.config' 'emysql.app' 'lager.app' 'sys.config' 'goldrush.app' 'ranch.app' )
    fun_set_db_user
    fun_ssl
    for v in ${tpls[*]};do
        file="${CODE_PATH}/server/tpl/${v}"
        if [ ! -e $file ]; then
            echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]謇ｾ荳榊芦讓｡譚ｿ譁・ｻｶ: ${file}"
            exit 1
        fi
        # 譖ｿ謐｢讓｡譚ｿ蜿倬㍼
        lines=$(<"${file}")
        for k in ${!cfg[@]}; do
            lines=${lines//\{\{$k\}\}/${cfg[$k]}}
        done
        # 譖ｿ謐｢遨ｺ蜿倬㍼蟷ｶ逕滓・譁・ｻｶ
        echo "${lines}" | sed -e "s/{{.*}}//" > ${v}
    done
    # 螳芽｣・焚謐ｮ蠎・    mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -e"create database ${cfg['db_name']}"
    if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]蛻帛ｻｺ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎捺慮蜿醍函蠑ょｸｸ"
        exit 1
    fi
    mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -e"source ${ROOT}/zone.sql"
    if [ -f "${CODE_PATH}/server/tpl/card.sql" ]; then 
        mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -e"source ${CODE_PATH}/server/tpl/card.sql"
    fi
    if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]蟇ｼ蜈･謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎楢｡ｨ扈捺桷譌ｶ蜿醍函蠑ょｸｸ"
        exit 1
    fi
    # 螟・炊逶ｸ蜈ｳ譁・ｻｶ
    mkdir -p dets var/sess log/pack log_file role_face_photo/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']} ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/role_face_photo
    mv env_zone.cfg env.cfg
    ln -s ${CODE_PATH}/web/www .
    # ln -s ${CODE_PATH}/server/priv .
    fun_face_link
    rm -f zone.sql
    chmod -R 777 ${cfg['root']}/var
    echo ">> 豁｣蝨ｨ謇灘ｼ遶ｯ蜿｣:${cfg['port']}・悟ｦよ棡螟ｱ雍･蜿ｯ謇句勘謫堺ｽ懷ｮ梧・・梧裏髴驥肴眠螳芽｣・
    iptables -A RH-Firewall-1-INPUT -p tcp -m state --state NEW -m tcp --dport ${cfg['port']} -j ACCEPT && service iptables save && service iptables restart && sysctl -p
    rm -f sys_notice.sql
    echo ">> 豁｣蝨ｨ驟咲ｽｮweb譛榊苅蝎ｨ・悟ｦよ棡螟ｱ雍･蜿ｯ謇句勘謫堺ｽ懷ｮ梧・・梧裏髴驥肴眠螳芽｣・
    rm -f ${VHOST_PATH}/${cfg['host']}.conf && ln -s ${cfg['root']}/vhost.conf ${VHOST_PATH}/${cfg['host']}.conf
    /root/nginx_reload
    echo ">> 闃らせ螳芽｣・ｮ梧・"
}

# 蛻髯､闃らせ
fun_uninstall(){
    fun_check_screen
    role_num=$(mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -ss -e"select count(*) from role")
    if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]隶ｿ髣ｮ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎灘､ｱ雍･・梧裏豕戊執蜿冶ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥・
        exit 1
    fi
    if [ "$role_num" != "" ] && [ "$role_num" = "$1" ]; then
        mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -e"drop database ${cfg['db_name']}"
        if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
            echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]隶ｿ髣ｮ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎灘､ｱ雍･・梧裏豕募唖髯､謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎難ｼ悟ｦる怙扈ｧ扈ｭ隸ｷ謇句勘謫堺ｽ・
            exit 1
        fi
        echo ">> 豁｣蝨ｨ蜈ｳ髣ｭ遶ｯ蜿｣:${cfg['port']}"
        iptables -D RH-Firewall-1-INPUT -p tcp -m state --state NEW -m tcp --dport ${cfg['port']} -j ACCEPT && service iptables save && service iptables restart && sysctl -p
        rm -rf screenlog.* face replay replay_share dets priv log log_file var www env.cfg env.php default.cfg.php emysql.app main.app vhost.conf start.sh ${VHOST_PATH}/${cfg['host']}.conf ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/face/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']} ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/replay/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']}

        echo ">> 闃らせ${cfg['nodename']}蟾ｲ扈丞唖髯､"
    else
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]蛻髯､蜿よ焚荳ｭ蠢・｡ｻ蟶ｦ譛牙ｽ灘燕闃らせ逧・ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥擾ｼ瑚ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥丈ｸｺ[${role_num}]"
    fi
}

# 辜ｭ譖ｴ譁ｰ闃らせ
fun_update_cfg(){
    # 螟・炊讓｡譚ｿ
    tpls=( 'env_zone.cfg' 'vhost.conf' 'elog.config' 'main.app' 'env.php' 'default.cfg.php' )
    fun_set_db_user
    fun_ssl
    for v in ${tpls[*]};do
        file="${CODE_PATH}/server/tpl/${v}"
        # 譖ｿ謐｢讓｡譚ｿ蜿倬㍼
        lines=$(<"${file}")
        for k in ${!cfg[@]}; do
            lines=${lines//\{\{$k\}\}/${cfg[$k]}}
        done
        # 譖ｿ謐｢遨ｺ蜿倬㍼蟷ｶ逕滓・譁・ｻｶ
        echo "${lines}" | sed -e "s/{{.*}}//" > ${v}
    done
    rm -f env.cfg && mv env_zone.cfg env.cfg
    rm -f www && ln -s ${CODE_PATH}/web/www .
    mkdir -p ${cfg['root']}/role_face_photo/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']} ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/role_face_photo
    fun_face_link
    /root/nginx_reload
    if [ $(cat /etc/sysconfig/iptables | grep -e "--dport ${cfg['port']}" | wc -l ) -eq 0 ]; then 
        iptables -A RH-Firewall-1-INPUT -p tcp -m state --state NEW -m tcp --dport ${cfg['port']} -j ACCEPT && service iptables save && service iptables restart && sysctl -p
    fi
    echo ">> 謇譛蛾・鄂ｮ譁・ｻｶ譖ｴ譁ｰ螳梧・"
    if [ "$1" != "false" ] && [ $(screen -ls | grep "${cfg['nodename']}" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ]; then 
        fun_exec "sys_env:reload:[]"
    fi
}

# 蛻帛ｻｺ謇譛牙・譛榊ｹｳ蜿ｰ逶ｮ蠖・fun_create_combine(){
    dir=${cfg['root']}/$1
    # cfg['combine']='{<<"4399">>,1},{<<"4399">>,2},{<<"4399">>,3},{<<"4399">>,4},{<<"4399">>,5},{<<"4399">>,6},{<<"4399">>,7},{<<"4399">>,8},{<<"4399">>,9}'
    combinestr=${cfg['combine']}
    combinestr=${combinestr//\ /}
    combinestr=${combinestr//\{\<\<\"/}
    combinestr=${combinestr//\"\>\>\,/\_}
    combinestr=${combinestr//\}\,/\ }
    combinestr=( ${combinestr//\}/} )
    for k in ${combinestr[@]}; do 
        # echo ${dir}/${k}
        mkdir -p ${dir}/${k}
    done
}

# 譖ｴ譁ｰface體ｾ謗･
fun_face_link(){
    for dir in $(ls ${cfg['root']}/role_face_photo); do 
        if [ -d "${cfg['root']}/role_face_photo/${dir}" ] && [ "${dir}" != "." ]; then 
            rm -f ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/role_face_photo/${dir}
            ln -s ${cfg['root']}/role_face_photo/${dir} ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/role_face_photo/${dir}
        fi
    done
}

# 辜ｭ譖ｴ譁ｰ闃らせ
fun_hotswap(){
    noshell=""  # shell蜷主床螟夂ｺｿ遞狗Ο譖ｴ隕∵ｱゆｸｺnoshell
    if [ "$1" != "" ]; then 
        noshell=" -noshell"
    fi
    ${ERL}${noshell} -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} -hidden -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name hotswap_${cfg['nodename']} -setcookie ${cfg['cookie']} -eval "io:setopts([{encoding,unicode}])" -s main hotswap_from_shell -extra ${cfg['nodename']}
    echo ">> 辜ｭ譖ｴ譁ｰ螳梧・"
}

# 謇ｧ陦梧欠螳夊・譛ｬ 
fun_exec(){
    # 隕∵ｱゆｼ蜈･譬ｼ蠑丈ｸｺ Mod:Fun:Args(荳ｭ髣ｴ荳榊ｸｦ遨ｺ譬ｼ隨ｦ蜿ｷ)
    line=$1
    mod=$(echo $line | cut -d':' -f1)
    fun=$(echo $line | cut -d':' -f2)
    args=$(echo $line | cut -d':' -f3)
    args=${args//\#/:}
    if [[ $(echo $mod | grep "^[a-z0-9_]\{1,50\}$") = "" ]]; then 
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]讓｡蝮怜錐譬ｼ蠑丈ｸ肴ｭ｣蟶ｸ・・{mod}"
        exit 1
    fi
    if [[ $(echo $fun | grep "^[a-z0-9_]\{1,50\}$") = "" ]]; then 
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]譁ｹ豕募錐譬ｼ蠑丈ｸ肴ｭ｣蟶ｸ・・{fun}"
        exit 1
    fi
    if [[ $(echo $args | grep "^\[.*\]$") = "" ]]; then 
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]譁ｹ豕募盾謨ｰ譬ｼ蠑丈ｸ肴ｭ｣蟶ｸ・・{args}"
        exit 1
    fi
    echo ">> 蜃・､・鴬陦梧婿豕戊ｰ・畑 apply(${mod}, ${fun}, ${args})"
    ${ERL} -noshell -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} -hidden -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name exec_${cfg['nodename']} -setcookie ${cfg['cookie']} -eval "io:setopts([{encoding,unicode}]),io:format(\"return:~w~n\", [rpc:call('${cfg['nodename']}', ${mod}, ${fun}, ${args})])" -s c q
}

# 貂・｡｣謫堺ｽ・fun_clean(){
    fun_check_screen
    role_num=$(mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -ss -e"select count(*) from role")
    if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]隶ｿ髣ｮ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎灘､ｱ雍･・梧裏豕戊執蜿冶ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥・
        exit 1
    fi
    if [ "$role_num" != "" ] && [ "$role_num" = "$1" ]; then
        # 謗帝勁蛻苓｡ｨ・梧ｳｨ諢丞惠謗帝勁蛻苓｡ｨ螟也噪謇譛芽｡ｨ蟆・ｼ夊｢ｫ貂・ｩｺ
        exclude=( "mod_notice" "mod_holiday_son" "mod_holiday_total" "mod_white_ip" "sys_notice_board" "mod_white_acc" )
        tabs=( $(mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -ss -e"show tables") )
        for v in ${tabs[@]}; do
            flag=0
            for v1 in ${exclude[@]}; do
                if [ "$v" = "$v1" ]; then
                    flag=1
                fi
            done
            if [ $flag = 0 ]; then
                sql="${sql} truncate table ${v};"
            fi
        done
        mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -ss -e"${sql}"
        if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
            echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]隶ｿ髣ｮ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎灘､ｱ雍･"
            exit 1
        fi
        rm -f ./env.dets
        mv dets/env.dets ./
        rm -f dets/*
        mv ./env.dets dets/
        rm -rf var/sess
        rm -rf log
        rm -rf log_file/*
        rm -rf lager_log/*
        rm -rf combat_replay/*
        rm -f screenlog.*
        rm -rf face/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']}/*
        rm -rf replay/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']}/*
        rm -rf replay_share/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']}/*
        mkdir -p log/pack
        mkdir -p var/sess
        chmod -R 777 var/sess
        echo "" > ./srv_clear_flag.txt
        echo ">> 貂・｡｣謫堺ｽ懷ｮ梧・"
    else
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]貂・｡｣譌ｶ蜿よ焚荳ｭ蠢・｡ｻ蟶ｦ譛牙ｽ灘燕闃らせ逧・ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥擾ｼ瑚ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥丈ｸｺ[${role_num}]"
    fi
}

# 蛻､譁ｭ霑占｡瑚鰍轤ｹ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎捺弍蜷ｦ霑伜ｭ伜惠 扈ｴ謚､霎・勧蟾･蜈ｷ
fun_run_db(){
    if [ $(screen -ls | grep "${cfg['nodename']}" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ]; then 
        if [ $(mysql  -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -e "show databases like '${cfg['db_name']}'" | wc -l ) -eq 0 ]; then 
            echo "譛榊苅蝎ｨ闃らせ[${cfg['nodename']}]霑伜惠霑占｡御ｸｭ・梧焚謐ｮ蠎甜${cfg['db_name']}]荳榊ｭ伜惠莠・
            exit 1
        fi
    fi
}

fun_check_screen() {
    if [ $(screen -ls | grep "${cfg['nodename']}" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ]; then 
        echo "譛榊苅蝎ｨ闃らせ[${cfg['nodename']}]霑伜惠霑占｡御ｸｭ・御ｸ崎・霑幄｡瑚ｯ･謫堺ｽ・
        exit 1
    fi
}

# 謾ｶ髮・ｹｶ謇灘桁譌･蠢玲枚莉ｶ
fun_pack(){
    cd ${ROOT}/log/pack 
    # 螯よ棡逶ｮ蠖暮撼遨ｺ蛻呵ｿ幄｡梧遠蛹・    if [ $(ls -al | wc -l) -gt 3 ]; then
        tar zcf ${ROOT}/log/log_${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']}_$(date +"%y%m%d%H%M%S").tar.gz *.txt && rm -f *.txt
        exit $? 
    fi
    echo "豐｡譛画枚莉ｶ蜿ｯ謇灘桁"
    exit 1
}

##
fun_ssl(){
    if [ "${cfg['platform']}" = "9388" ]; then
        cfg['ssl']="listen 443 ssl;
    ssl on;
    ssl_certificate_key /usr/local/webserver/nginx/conf/wildcard_20131129.key;
    ssl_certificate /usr/local/webserver/nginx/conf/wildcard.pem;"
    fi
}

## 譽豬区弍蜷ｦ蝨ｨcygwin邇ｯ蠅・ｸｭ
in_cygwin(){
    os=$(uname)
    [[ "${os:0:3}" == "CYG" ]]; return $?
}

## 蜻ｽ莉､陦悟ｸｮ蜉ｩ
help(){
    echo "start             蜷ｯ蜉ｨ闃らせ"
    echo "stop              蜈ｳ髣ｭ闃らせ(蜷梧慮莨夊・蜉ｨ貂・炊php session)"
    echo "shell             霑帛・謗ｧ蛻ｶ蜿ｰ・御ｹ溷庄菴ｿ逕ｨscreen蜻ｽ莉､逶ｴ謗･霑帛・"
    echo "remsh             莉･remsh譁ｹ蠑剰ｿ帛・謗ｧ蛻ｶ蜿ｰ"
    echo "install           螳芽｣・鰍轤ｹ"
    echo "uninstall         蛻髯､闃らせ"
    echo "clean             貂・｡｣謫堺ｽ・
    echo "clean_sess        貂・炊php session・悟・髣ｭ譛榊苅蝎ｨ蜷朱怙貂・炊・御ｸ咲┯邇ｩ螳ｶ蛻ｷ譁ｰ蜷主庄閭ｽ霑帛・譛榊苅蝎ｨ"
    echo "update_cfg        譖ｴ譁ｰ謇譛蛾・鄂ｮ譁・ｻｶ"
    echo "hotswap           辜ｭ譖ｴ譁ｰ闃らせ"
    echo "exec              謇ｧ陦梧欠螳壼多莉､: Mod:Fun:Args"
    echo "pack              謾ｶ髮・ｹｶ謇灘桁譌･蠢玲枚莉ｶ"
    echo "log               譟･逵区而蛻ｶ蜿ｰ譌･蠢・
    echo "check             蛻､譁ｭ蛻・せ譏ｯ蜷ｦ霑伜惠霑占｡御ｸｭ"
    echo "ls                蛻怜・謇譛臥噪erl闃らせ"
    echo "run_db            譽譟･闃らせ譏ｯ蜷ｦ蝨ｨ霑占｡檎憾諤∽ｽ・焚謐ｮ蠎灘ｷｲ陲ｫ蛻髯､(扈ｴ謚､霎・勧蟾･蜈ｷ)"
}

# ------------------------------------------------------
# 謇ｧ陦悟・蜿｣
# ------------------------------------------------------
declare -A cfg
init
case $1 in
    start) fun_start;;
    stop)  fun_stop;;
    shell) fun_shell;;
    remsh) fun_remsh $2;;
    install) fun_install;;
    uninstall) fun_uninstall $2;;
    clean) fun_clean $2;;
    clean_sess) fun_clean_sess;;
    update_cfg) fun_update_cfg $2;;
    hotswap) fun_hotswap $2;;
    exec) fun_exec "${2}";;
    pack) fun_pack;;
    check) fun_check_screen;;
    log) less ./screenlog.0;;
    ls) screen -ls;;
    nodo) exit 0;;
    create_dir) fun_create_combine;;
    run_db) fun_run_db;;
    *)
        echo "譛ｪ遏･謖・ｻ､・瑚ｯｷ菴ｿ逕ｨ莉･荳区怏謨域欠莉､"
        echo "----------------------------------------------------------"
        help
        exit 1
        ;;
esac
exit 0

964 /data/zone/sszg_symlf_2/vhost.conf
server {
    listen       ;
    
    server_name  192.168.1.234:82: ;
    index index.html index.htm index.php;
    root  /data/zone/sszg_symlf_2/www;
    #autoindex on;
    charset utf-8;
    #expires off;
    access_log off;

    location ~.*\.(swf|xml|mp3|png|jpg|gif|data)$ {
        expires       max;
    }
    location ~.*\.(css|js|mx)$ {
        expires       96h;
    }
    location ~ /.svn/ {
        deny all;
    }
    location ~ .php { # 荳ｺ莠・髪謖｝athinfo・碁怙蟆・桁蜷ｫ.php逧ФRL莠､扈冉astcgi隗｣譫撰ｼ悟酔譌ｶ髴隶ｾ鄂ｮphp.ini荳ｭ逧・gi.fix_pathinfo=1
        expires off;
        #access_log off;
        access_log  /data/logs/192.168.1.234:82_.log json;

        include fastcgi.conf;
        fastcgi_pass   unix:/tmp/php-cgi-70.sock;
        fastcgi_index index.php;
        fastcgi_param ROOT /data/zone/sszg_symlf_2; # 霑吩ｸｪ邇ｯ蠅・序驥城怙莨蜈･・悟凄蛻冽eb邉ｻ扈滓裏豕墓ｭ｣蟶ｸ蟾･菴・        fastcgi_param PATH_INFO $fastcgi_script_name;
    }
}

#!/bin/bash
cd /data/zone/sszg_symlf_3
ulimit -SHn 102400
erl -kernel inet_dist_listen_min 40100 -kernel inet_dist_listen_max 44000 +P 204800 +K true -smp enable -hidden -config sys.config -pa /data/sszg_code//v200408/server/ebin -name sszg_local_3@127.0.0.1 -s main start -extra zone

===== FILE_BEGIN: /data/zone/sszg_symlf_3/ctl.sh =====
22591 /data/zone/sszg_symlf_3/ctl.sh
#/bin/bash
# ---------------------------------------------------------
# 闃らせ邂｡逅・ｷ･蜈ｷ
# @author yeahoo2000@gmail.com
# ---------------------------------------------------------

DEBUG=1 # 蠑蜿第ｨ｡蠑剰ｮｾ鄂ｮ(0:蜈ｳ髣ｭ 1:蠑蜷ｯ)

# 蛻晏ｧ句喧
init(){
    cfg_init
    cfg_check

    # 闃らせ蟾･菴懃岼蠖・    ROOT=${cfg['root']}
    # 莉｣遐∝ｺ捺園螟・岼蠖包ｼ御ｾ・ /data/game.dev
    CODE_PATH=${cfg['code_path']}/${cfg['ver']}

    # nginx陌壽供荳ｻ譛ｺ驟咲ｽｮ蟄俶叛逶ｮ蠖・    if [ -d "/data/conf/nginx/vhost" ]; then
        VHOST_PATH="/data/conf/nginx/vhost"
    else
        VHOST_PATH="/usr/local/webserver/nginx/conf/vhost"
    fi
    if [ "${cfg['combine']}" = "" ]; then
        cfg['combine']='<<"local_3">>'
    fi
    # VHOST_PATH="/etc/nginx/sites-enabled"
    if [ "${cfg['combine']}" = "" ]; then
        cfg['combine']='<<"local_3">>'
    fi

    # erl遞句ｺ乗園蝨ｨ霍ｯ蠕・    ERL=erl
    if [ -f /opt/erlang/otp19.2/lib/erlang/bin/erl ]; then
        ERL=/opt/erlang/otp19.2/lib/erlang/bin/erl
    elif [ -f /usr/local/erlang19/bin/erl ]; then
        ERL=/usr/local/erlang19/bin/erl
    fi
    # erl闃らせ髣ｴ霑樊磁遶ｯ蜿｣闌・峩
    ERL_PORT_MIN=40100
    # erl闃らせ髣ｴ霑樊磁遶ｯ蜿｣闌・峩
    ERL_PORT_MAX=44000

    # 霑帛・闃らせ譬ｹ逶ｮ蠖・    cd ${ROOT} || exit 1
}

# 驟咲ｽｮ菫｡諱ｯ譽譟･
cfg_check(){
    if [ -e "${cfg['root']}" ]; then
        return
    else if [ "${DEBUG}" != "1" ]; then
            echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]閼壽悽譛ｪ霑幄｡梧ｭ｣遑ｮ驟咲ｽｮ・悟ｦよ棡蟶梧悍莉･蠑蜿第ｨ｡蠑丞ｷ･菴懆ｯｷ蟆・EBUG隶ｾ鄂ｮ荳ｺ'1'・悟ｹｶ蝪ｫ蜀吝・髞ｮ驟咲ｽｮ"
            exit 1
        fi
    fi
    # 闔ｷ蜿門ｽ灘燕閼壽悽譁・ｻｶ謇蝨ｨ霍ｯ蠕・ｼ御ｽ應ｸｺ譬ｹ逶ｮ蠖・    SOURCE="${BASH_SOURCE[0]}"
    while [ -h "$SOURCE" ]; do
[REDACTED SENSITIVE LINE]
        SOURCE="$(readlink "$SOURCE")"
        [[ $SOURCE != /* ]] && SOURCE="$DIR/$SOURCE"
    done
[REDACTED SENSITIVE LINE]

    # 隹・ｯ墓ｨ｡蠑丈ｸ狗噪鮟倩ｮ､驟咲ｽｮ・悟ｦよ怏蠢・ｦ∬ｯｷ謇句勘菫ｮ謾ｹ
    # cygwin荳矩怙菴ｿ逕ｨdos譬ｼ蠑乗燕閭ｽ豁｣蟶ｸ・悟ｦ・d:/game.dev/zone/mxfx_local_1
    cfg['root']="${root}"

# 蜷ｯ蜉ｨ闃らせ
fun_start(){
    if [ ! -e dets ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]蠖灘燕逶ｮ蠖穂ｸ区悴螳芽｣・鰍轤ｹ・瑚ｯｷ蜈域鴬陦悟ｮ芽｣・桃菴・
        exit 1
    fi
    if [ $(screen -ls | grep "${cfg['nodename']}" | wc -l ) -le 0 ]; then  #蛛懈恪譖ｴ譁ｰ譌ｶ蟇ｹ譌･蠢怜★謳ｬ遘ｻ螟・炊
        if [ $(cat screenlog.0 | wc -l ) -gt 200000 ]; then 
            echo ">> screenlog.0譌･蠢怜､・ｻｽ貂・炊螟・炊"
            rm -f screenlog.1
            mv screenlog.0 screenlog.1
        fi
    fi
    if $(in_cygwin); then
        werl -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} +P 204800 +K true -smp enable -hidden  -config sys.config -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name ${cfg['nodename']} -s main start -extra ${cfg['type']} &
    else
        start_file=${ROOT}/start.sh
        CMD="${ERL} -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} +P 204800 +K true -smp enable -hidden -config sys.config -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name ${cfg['nodename']} -s main start -extra ${cfg['type']}"
        cat > ${start_file} <<EOF
#!/bin/bash
cd ${ROOT}
ulimit -SHn 102400
${CMD}
EOF
        chmod +x ${start_file}
        screen -dmSL ${cfg['nodename']} -s ${start_file}
        echo ">> 闃らせ ${cfg['nodename']} 豁｣蝨ｨ蜷ｯ蜉ｨ荳ｭ・悟ｦよ棡諠ｳ隗ょｯ溷星蜉ｨ霑・ｨ玖ｯｷ菴ｿ逕ｨ莉･荳句多莉､霑幄｡悟星蜉ｨ:"
        echo ">> ./ctl.sh start && ./ctl.sh shell"
    fi
}

# 貂・炊session
fun_clean_sess(){
    rm -rf var/sess/*
    echo "-------------------------------------------"
    echo ">> php session貂・炊螳梧・"
}

# 蜈ｳ髣ｭ闃らせ
fun_stop(){
    if $(in_cygwin); then
        echo ">> cygwin荳倶ｸ肴髪謖∵ｭ､蜻ｽ莉､"
        exit
    fi
    ${ERL} -noshell -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} -hidden -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name stop_${cfg['nodename']} -setcookie ${cfg['cookie']} -eval "io:setopts([{encoding,unicode}])" -s main stop_from_shell -extra ${cfg['nodename']}
    fun_clean_sess
    echo "-------------------------------------------"
    num=0
    while(( $num<30 )); do
        if [ $(screen -ls | grep "${cfg['nodename']}" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ]; then 
            log=$(tail -n 10 ${ROOT}/screenlog.0)
            if [ $(echo $log | grep "type: temporary" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ] || [ $(echo $log | grep "Application inets exited with reason: stopped" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ] || [ $(echo $log | grep "Application main exited with reason: stopped" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ]; then
                pid=`ps aux | grep "\-name ${cfg['nodename']}"|grep erl|awk '{print $2}'` 
                if [ "$pid" != "" ]; then kill -9 $pid && echo "闃らせ[${cfg['nodename']}]蟾ｲ蜈ｳ髣ｭ・檎峩謗･kill謗芽ｿ帷ｨ・$log"; fi
                exit 0
            fi
            sleep 0.5
            let "num++"
        else
            echo ">> 闃らせ ${cfg['nodename']} 蜈ｳ髣ｭ螳梧・"
            exit 0
        fi
    done
    echo -e ">> 闃らせ ${cfg['nodename']} \e[91m譌豕募・髣ｭ闃らせ・瑚鰍轤ｹ霑伜惠豁｣蟶ｸ霑占｡御ｸｭ...\e[0;0m"
}

# 菴ｿ逕ｨremsh譁ｹ蠑剰ｿ帛・謗ｧ蛻ｶ蜿ｰ
fun_remsh(){
    n=remsh_$1_${cfg['nodename']}
    ${ERL} -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} -hidden -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name $n -setcookie ${cfg['cookie']} -remsh ${cfg['nodename']}
}

# 霑帛・隸･闃らせ逧・而蛻ｶ蜿ｰ
fun_shell(){
    if $(in_cygwin); then
        echo ">> cygwin荳倶ｸ肴髪謖∵ｭ､蜻ｽ莉､"
        exit
    fi
    screen -r "${cfg['nodename']}"
}

# 蛻ｷ譁ｰ逕ｨ謌ｷ蜷・
fun_set_db_user(){
    if [ "${cfg['db_user']}" != "root" ]; then
        echo "譖ｴ譁ｰ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎甜${cfg['db_name']}]逕ｨ謌ｷ[${cfg['db_user']}]譚・剞"
        sql="GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON ${cfg['db_name']}.* TO '${cfg['db_user']}'@'127.0.0.1' IDENTIFIED BY '${cfg['db_pass']}' WITH GRANT OPTION;"
        sql="$sql GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON ${cfg['db_name']}.* TO '${cfg['db_user']}'@'localhost' IDENTIFIED BY '${cfg['db_pass']}' WITH GRANT OPTION;"
        sql="$sql FLUSH PRIVILEGES;"
        mysql -uroot -p$(cat /data/save/mysql_root) -e "$sql"
    fi
}

# 螳芽｣・鰍轤ｹ
fun_install(){
    if [ -e dets ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]蠖灘燕謨ｰ謐ｮ逶ｮ蠖暮撼遨ｺ・悟庄閭ｽ譏ｯ蟾ｲ扈乗鴬陦瑚ｿ・ｮ芽｣・ｿ・ｨ具ｼ瑚ｯｷ蛻髯､蜷朱㍾隸・
        exit 1
    fi
    # 螟・炊讓｡譚ｿ
    tpls=( 'env_zone.cfg' 'vhost.conf' 'main.app' 'env.php' 'default.cfg.php' 'zone.sql' 'elog.config' 'emysql.app' 'lager.app' 'sys.config' 'goldrush.app' 'ranch.app' )
    fun_set_db_user
    fun_ssl
    for v in ${tpls[*]};do
        file="${CODE_PATH}/server/tpl/${v}"
        if [ ! -e $file ]; then
            echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]謇ｾ荳榊芦讓｡譚ｿ譁・ｻｶ: ${file}"
            exit 1
        fi
        # 譖ｿ謐｢讓｡譚ｿ蜿倬㍼
        lines=$(<"${file}")
        for k in ${!cfg[@]}; do
            lines=${lines//\{\{$k\}\}/${cfg[$k]}}
        done
        # 譖ｿ謐｢遨ｺ蜿倬㍼蟷ｶ逕滓・譁・ｻｶ
        echo "${lines}" | sed -e "s/{{.*}}//" > ${v}
    done
    # 螳芽｣・焚謐ｮ蠎・    mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -e"create database ${cfg['db_name']}"
    if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]蛻帛ｻｺ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎捺慮蜿醍函蠑ょｸｸ"
        exit 1
    fi
    mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -e"source ${ROOT}/zone.sql"
    if [ -f "${CODE_PATH}/server/tpl/card.sql" ]; then 
        mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -e"source ${CODE_PATH}/server/tpl/card.sql"
    fi
    if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]蟇ｼ蜈･謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎楢｡ｨ扈捺桷譌ｶ蜿醍函蠑ょｸｸ"
        exit 1
    fi
    # 螟・炊逶ｸ蜈ｳ譁・ｻｶ
    mkdir -p dets var/sess log/pack log_file role_face_photo/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']} ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/role_face_photo
    mv env_zone.cfg env.cfg
    ln -s ${CODE_PATH}/web/www .
    # ln -s ${CODE_PATH}/server/priv .
    fun_face_link
    rm -f zone.sql
    chmod -R 777 ${cfg['root']}/var
    echo ">> 豁｣蝨ｨ謇灘ｼ遶ｯ蜿｣:${cfg['port']}・悟ｦよ棡螟ｱ雍･蜿ｯ謇句勘謫堺ｽ懷ｮ梧・・梧裏髴驥肴眠螳芽｣・
    iptables -A RH-Firewall-1-INPUT -p tcp -m state --state NEW -m tcp --dport ${cfg['port']} -j ACCEPT && service iptables save && service iptables restart && sysctl -p
    rm -f sys_notice.sql
    echo ">> 豁｣蝨ｨ驟咲ｽｮweb譛榊苅蝎ｨ・悟ｦよ棡螟ｱ雍･蜿ｯ謇句勘謫堺ｽ懷ｮ梧・・梧裏髴驥肴眠螳芽｣・
    rm -f ${VHOST_PATH}/${cfg['host']}.conf && ln -s ${cfg['root']}/vhost.conf ${VHOST_PATH}/${cfg['host']}.conf
    /root/nginx_reload
    echo ">> 闃らせ螳芽｣・ｮ梧・"
}

# 蛻髯､闃らせ
fun_uninstall(){
    fun_check_screen
    role_num=$(mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -ss -e"select count(*) from role")
    if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]隶ｿ髣ｮ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎灘､ｱ雍･・梧裏豕戊執蜿冶ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥・
        exit 1
    fi
    if [ "$role_num" != "" ] && [ "$role_num" = "$1" ]; then
        mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -e"drop database ${cfg['db_name']}"
        if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
            echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]隶ｿ髣ｮ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎灘､ｱ雍･・梧裏豕募唖髯､謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎難ｼ悟ｦる怙扈ｧ扈ｭ隸ｷ謇句勘謫堺ｽ・
            exit 1
        fi
        echo ">> 豁｣蝨ｨ蜈ｳ髣ｭ遶ｯ蜿｣:${cfg['port']}"
        iptables -D RH-Firewall-1-INPUT -p tcp -m state --state NEW -m tcp --dport ${cfg['port']} -j ACCEPT && service iptables save && service iptables restart && sysctl -p
        rm -rf screenlog.* face replay replay_share dets priv log log_file var www env.cfg env.php default.cfg.php emysql.app main.app vhost.conf start.sh ${VHOST_PATH}/${cfg['host']}.conf ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/face/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']} ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/replay/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']}

        echo ">> 闃らせ${cfg['nodename']}蟾ｲ扈丞唖髯､"
    else
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]蛻髯､蜿よ焚荳ｭ蠢・｡ｻ蟶ｦ譛牙ｽ灘燕闃らせ逧・ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥擾ｼ瑚ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥丈ｸｺ[${role_num}]"
    fi
}

# 辜ｭ譖ｴ譁ｰ闃らせ
fun_update_cfg(){
    # 螟・炊讓｡譚ｿ
    tpls=( 'env_zone.cfg' 'vhost.conf' 'elog.config' 'main.app' 'env.php' 'default.cfg.php' )
    fun_set_db_user
    fun_ssl
    for v in ${tpls[*]};do
        file="${CODE_PATH}/server/tpl/${v}"
        # 譖ｿ謐｢讓｡譚ｿ蜿倬㍼
        lines=$(<"${file}")
        for k in ${!cfg[@]}; do
            lines=${lines//\{\{$k\}\}/${cfg[$k]}}
        done
        # 譖ｿ謐｢遨ｺ蜿倬㍼蟷ｶ逕滓・譁・ｻｶ
        echo "${lines}" | sed -e "s/{{.*}}//" > ${v}
    done
    rm -f env.cfg && mv env_zone.cfg env.cfg
    rm -f www && ln -s ${CODE_PATH}/web/www .
    mkdir -p ${cfg['root']}/role_face_photo/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']} ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/role_face_photo
    fun_face_link
    /root/nginx_reload
    if [ $(cat /etc/sysconfig/iptables | grep -e "--dport ${cfg['port']}" | wc -l ) -eq 0 ]; then 
        iptables -A RH-Firewall-1-INPUT -p tcp -m state --state NEW -m tcp --dport ${cfg['port']} -j ACCEPT && service iptables save && service iptables restart && sysctl -p
    fi
    echo ">> 謇譛蛾・鄂ｮ譁・ｻｶ譖ｴ譁ｰ螳梧・"
    if [ "$1" != "false" ] && [ $(screen -ls | grep "${cfg['nodename']}" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ]; then 
        fun_exec "sys_env:reload:[]"
    fi
}

# 蛻帛ｻｺ謇譛牙・譛榊ｹｳ蜿ｰ逶ｮ蠖・fun_create_combine(){
    dir=${cfg['root']}/$1
    # cfg['combine']='{<<"4399">>,1},{<<"4399">>,2},{<<"4399">>,3},{<<"4399">>,4},{<<"4399">>,5},{<<"4399">>,6},{<<"4399">>,7},{<<"4399">>,8},{<<"4399">>,9}'
    combinestr=${cfg['combine']}
    combinestr=${combinestr//\ /}
    combinestr=${combinestr//\{\<\<\"/}
    combinestr=${combinestr//\"\>\>\,/\_}
    combinestr=${combinestr//\}\,/\ }
    combinestr=( ${combinestr//\}/} )
    for k in ${combinestr[@]}; do 
        # echo ${dir}/${k}
        mkdir -p ${dir}/${k}
    done
}

# 譖ｴ譁ｰface體ｾ謗･
fun_face_link(){
    for dir in $(ls ${cfg['root']}/role_face_photo); do 
        if [ -d "${cfg['root']}/role_face_photo/${dir}" ] && [ "${dir}" != "." ]; then 
            rm -f ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/role_face_photo/${dir}
            ln -s ${cfg['root']}/role_face_photo/${dir} ${CODE_PATH}/web/www/role_face_photo/${dir}
        fi
    done
}

# 辜ｭ譖ｴ譁ｰ闃らせ
fun_hotswap(){
    noshell=""  # shell蜷主床螟夂ｺｿ遞狗Ο譖ｴ隕∵ｱゆｸｺnoshell
    if [ "$1" != "" ]; then 
        noshell=" -noshell"
    fi
    ${ERL}${noshell} -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} -hidden -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name hotswap_${cfg['nodename']} -setcookie ${cfg['cookie']} -eval "io:setopts([{encoding,unicode}])" -s main hotswap_from_shell -extra ${cfg['nodename']}
    echo ">> 辜ｭ譖ｴ譁ｰ螳梧・"
}

# 謇ｧ陦梧欠螳夊・譛ｬ 
fun_exec(){
    # 隕∵ｱゆｼ蜈･譬ｼ蠑丈ｸｺ Mod:Fun:Args(荳ｭ髣ｴ荳榊ｸｦ遨ｺ譬ｼ隨ｦ蜿ｷ)
    line=$1
    mod=$(echo $line | cut -d':' -f1)
    fun=$(echo $line | cut -d':' -f2)
    args=$(echo $line | cut -d':' -f3)
    args=${args//\#/:}
    if [[ $(echo $mod | grep "^[a-z0-9_]\{1,50\}$") = "" ]]; then 
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]讓｡蝮怜錐譬ｼ蠑丈ｸ肴ｭ｣蟶ｸ・・{mod}"
        exit 1
    fi
    if [[ $(echo $fun | grep "^[a-z0-9_]\{1,50\}$") = "" ]]; then 
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]譁ｹ豕募錐譬ｼ蠑丈ｸ肴ｭ｣蟶ｸ・・{fun}"
        exit 1
    fi
    if [[ $(echo $args | grep "^\[.*\]$") = "" ]]; then 
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]譁ｹ豕募盾謨ｰ譬ｼ蠑丈ｸ肴ｭ｣蟶ｸ・・{args}"
        exit 1
    fi
    echo ">> 蜃・､・鴬陦梧婿豕戊ｰ・畑 apply(${mod}, ${fun}, ${args})"
    ${ERL} -noshell -kernel inet_dist_listen_min ${ERL_PORT_MIN} -kernel inet_dist_listen_max ${ERL_PORT_MAX} -hidden -pa ${CODE_PATH}/server/ebin -name exec_${cfg['nodename']} -setcookie ${cfg['cookie']} -eval "io:setopts([{encoding,unicode}]),io:format(\"return:~w~n\", [rpc:call('${cfg['nodename']}', ${mod}, ${fun}, ${args})])" -s c q
}

# 貂・｡｣謫堺ｽ・fun_clean(){
    fun_check_screen
    role_num=$(mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -ss -e"select count(*) from role")
    if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]隶ｿ髣ｮ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎灘､ｱ雍･・梧裏豕戊執蜿冶ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥・
        exit 1
    fi
    if [ "$role_num" != "" ] && [ "$role_num" = "$1" ]; then
        # 謗帝勁蛻苓｡ｨ・梧ｳｨ諢丞惠謗帝勁蛻苓｡ｨ螟也噪謇譛芽｡ｨ蟆・ｼ夊｢ｫ貂・ｩｺ
        exclude=( "mod_notice" "mod_holiday_son" "mod_holiday_total" "mod_white_ip" "sys_notice_board" "mod_white_acc" )
        tabs=( $(mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -ss -e"show tables") )
        for v in ${tabs[@]}; do
            flag=0
            for v1 in ${exclude[@]}; do
                if [ "$v" = "$v1" ]; then
                    flag=1
                fi
            done
            if [ $flag = 0 ]; then
                sql="${sql} truncate table ${v};"
            fi
        done
        mysql -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -D${cfg['db_name']} -ss -e"${sql}"
        if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
            echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]隶ｿ髣ｮ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎灘､ｱ雍･"
            exit 1
        fi
        rm -f ./env.dets
        mv dets/env.dets ./
        rm -f dets/*
        mv ./env.dets dets/
        rm -rf var/sess
        rm -rf log
        rm -rf log_file/*
        rm -rf lager_log/*
        rm -rf combat_replay/*
        rm -f screenlog.*
        rm -rf face/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']}/*
        rm -rf replay/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']}/*
        rm -rf replay_share/${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']}/*
        mkdir -p log/pack
        mkdir -p var/sess
        chmod -R 777 var/sess
        echo "" > ./srv_clear_flag.txt
        echo ">> 貂・｡｣謫堺ｽ懷ｮ梧・"
    else
        echo ">> [髞呵ｯｯ]貂・｡｣譌ｶ蜿よ焚荳ｭ蠢・｡ｻ蟶ｦ譛牙ｽ灘燕闃らせ逧・ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥擾ｼ瑚ｧ定牡謨ｰ驥丈ｸｺ[${role_num}]"
    fi
}

# 蛻､譁ｭ霑占｡瑚鰍轤ｹ謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎捺弍蜷ｦ霑伜ｭ伜惠 扈ｴ謚､霎・勧蟾･蜈ｷ
fun_run_db(){
    if [ $(screen -ls | grep "${cfg['nodename']}" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ]; then 
        if [ $(mysql  -h${cfg['db_host']} -P${cfg['db_port']} -u${cfg['db_user']} -p${cfg['db_pass']} -e "show databases like '${cfg['db_name']}'" | wc -l ) -eq 0 ]; then 
            echo "譛榊苅蝎ｨ闃らせ[${cfg['nodename']}]霑伜惠霑占｡御ｸｭ・梧焚謐ｮ蠎甜${cfg['db_name']}]荳榊ｭ伜惠莠・
            exit 1
        fi
    fi
}

fun_check_screen() {
    if [ $(screen -ls | grep "${cfg['nodename']}" | wc -l ) -gt 0 ]; then 
        echo "譛榊苅蝎ｨ闃らせ[${cfg['nodename']}]霑伜惠霑占｡御ｸｭ・御ｸ崎・霑幄｡瑚ｯ･謫堺ｽ・
        exit 1
    fi
}

# 謾ｶ髮・ｹｶ謇灘桁譌･蠢玲枚莉ｶ
fun_pack(){
    cd ${ROOT}/log/pack 
    # 螯よ棡逶ｮ蠖暮撼遨ｺ蛻呵ｿ幄｡梧遠蛹・    if [ $(ls -al | wc -l) -gt 3 ]; then
        tar zcf ${ROOT}/log/log_${cfg['platform']}_${cfg['zone_id']}_$(date +"%y%m%d%H%M%S").tar.gz *.txt && rm -f *.txt
        exit $? 
    fi
    echo "豐｡譛画枚莉ｶ蜿ｯ謇灘桁"
    exit 1
}

##
fun_ssl(){
    if [ "${cfg['platform']}" = "9388" ]; then
        cfg['ssl']="listen 443 ssl;
    ssl on;
    ssl_certificate_key /usr/local/webserver/nginx/conf/wildcard_20131129.key;
    ssl_certificate /usr/local/webserver/nginx/conf/wildcard.pem;"
    fi
}

## 譽豬区弍蜷ｦ蝨ｨcygwin邇ｯ蠅・ｸｭ
in_cygwin(){
    os=$(uname)
    [[ "${os:0:3}" == "CYG" ]]; return $?
}

## 蜻ｽ莉､陦悟ｸｮ蜉ｩ
help(){
    echo "start             蜷ｯ蜉ｨ闃らせ"
    echo "stop              蜈ｳ髣ｭ闃らせ(蜷梧慮莨夊・蜉ｨ貂・炊php session)"
    echo "shell             霑帛・謗ｧ蛻ｶ蜿ｰ・御ｹ溷庄菴ｿ逕ｨscreen蜻ｽ莉､逶ｴ謗･霑帛・"
    echo "remsh             莉･remsh譁ｹ蠑剰ｿ帛・謗ｧ蛻ｶ蜿ｰ"
    echo "install           螳芽｣・鰍轤ｹ"
    echo "uninstall         蛻髯､闃らせ"
    echo "clean             貂・｡｣謫堺ｽ・
    echo "clean_sess        貂・炊php session・悟・髣ｭ譛榊苅蝎ｨ蜷朱怙貂・炊・御ｸ咲┯邇ｩ螳ｶ蛻ｷ譁ｰ蜷主庄閭ｽ霑帛・譛榊苅蝎ｨ"
    echo "update_cfg        譖ｴ譁ｰ謇譛蛾・鄂ｮ譁・ｻｶ"
    echo "hotswap           辜ｭ譖ｴ譁ｰ闃らせ"
    echo "exec              謇ｧ陦梧欠螳壼多莉､: Mod:Fun:Args"
    echo "pack              謾ｶ髮・ｹｶ謇灘桁譌･蠢玲枚莉ｶ"
    echo "log               譟･逵区而蛻ｶ蜿ｰ譌･蠢・
    echo "check             蛻､譁ｭ蛻・せ譏ｯ蜷ｦ霑伜惠霑占｡御ｸｭ"
    echo "ls                蛻怜・謇譛臥噪erl闃らせ"
    echo "run_db            譽譟･闃らせ譏ｯ蜷ｦ蝨ｨ霑占｡檎憾諤∽ｽ・焚謐ｮ蠎灘ｷｲ陲ｫ蛻髯､(扈ｴ謚､霎・勧蟾･蜈ｷ)"
}

# ------------------------------------------------------
# 謇ｧ陦悟・蜿｣
# ------------------------------------------------------
declare -A cfg
init
case $1 in
    start) fun_start;;
    stop)  fun_stop;;
    shell) fun_shell;;
    remsh) fun_remsh $2;;
    install) fun_install;;
    uninstall) fun_uninstall $2;;
    clean) fun_clean $2;;
    clean_sess) fun_clean_sess;;
    update_cfg) fun_update_cfg $2;;
    hotswap) fun_hotswap $2;;
    exec) fun_exec "${2}";;
    pack) fun_pack;;
    check) fun_check_screen;;
    log) less ./screenlog.0;;
    ls) screen -ls;;
    nodo) exit 0;;
    create_dir) fun_create_combine;;
    run_db) fun_run_db;;
    *)
        echo "譛ｪ遏･謖・ｻ､・瑚ｯｷ菴ｿ逕ｨ莉･荳区怏謨域欠莉､"
        echo "----------------------------------------------------------"
        help
        exit 1
        ;;
esac
exit 0

880 /data/zone/sszg_symlf_3/sys.config
%% -*- erlang -*-
%% Thanks to Ulf Wiger at Ericcson for these comments:
%%
%% This file is identified via the erl command line option -config File.
%% Note that File should have no extension, e.g.
%% erl -config .../sys (if this file is called sys.config)
%%
%% In this file, you can redefine application environment variables.
%% This way, you don't have to modify the .app files of e.g. OTP applications.
[
    {lager, [
            {handlers, [
                    {lager_console_backend,[info,{lager_default_formatter, [date, " ", time, " [",severity,"] ", pid, module, ":", function, ":", line, " ", message, "\n"]}]},
                    {lager_file_backend, [{file, "lager_log/error.log"}, {level, error},{count,10}]},
                    {lager_file_backend, [{file, "lager_log/console.log"}, {level, info},{count,10}]}
                ]
            }
        ]
    }
].
